Abdullah,Jeremy Favaro,Mason Footh,Helen Fraser,Yuming Fu,Annie Giman,Nicolas Gomez,Massimo Griggio,Peter Gwartney,Aryana Haghjoo,Fabian Hervas-Peters,Jiamu Huang,Sandra Jaison,Jeyhan S. Kartaltepe,Sarah Kendrew,Ivan Kramarenko,Sahana Kumar,Zhaoran Liu,Arianna S. Long,Sangeeta Malhotra,Faezeh Manesh,Jasleen Matharu,Vihang Mehta,Edward M. Molter,Ivelina Momcheva,Zhiwei Pan,Yuxuan Pang,Pallavi Patil,Rachel Plesha,Marc Rafelski,Mitchell Revalski,Jyotika Roychowdhury,Gregory Rudnick,Swetha Sankar,Maya Seagraves,Lu Shen,Sabnam Shrestha,Manuchehr Taghizadeh-Popp,Jo Taylor,Wei Leong Tee,Scott Tompkins,Feige Wang,Xin Wang,Isak Wold,Jerry J. -Y. Zhang,Qianqiao Zhou,Zihao Zuo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文总结了关于宽视场无狭缝光谱学（WFSS）的研讨会成果，强调其在瞬变现象、星系演化及稀有天体研究中的潜力，并指出多任务协同、先进建模与机器学习、社区基础设施建设及对早期职业科研人员的认可等关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对宇宙方差问题，充分发挥宽视field无狭缝光谱学（WFSS）在多时代空间望远镜（如HST、JWST、Euclid、Roman等）中的科学潜力。

Method: 通过组织专题研讨会，汇集多方专家，系统梳理WFSS在科学目标、观测与数据处理挑战、提取工具及未来机遇等方面的进展与共识。

Result: 识别出WFSS在瞬变源、星系演化和稀有天体研究中的变革性能力；明确了Euclid/Roman/Rubin/JWST等多平台协同分析路径；提出需发展前向建模与物理引导的机器学习方法以应对谱重叠与大数据挑战；呼吁构建社区级基础设施与公平认可机制。

Conclusion: WFSS的全面成功依赖技术、协作与制度三方面协同推进：需强化跨学科合作、建设云化分析生态、开发科学就绪数据产品，并改革学术评价体系以支持软件与标定工作。

Abstract: We summarize strategies, lessons learned, and future directions from the Space Telescope Science Institute workshop Surveying the Universe in 4D: Beating Cosmic Variance with Wide-Field Slitless Spectroscopy from HST, JWST, Euclid, Roman, and Beyond, held August 24--28, 2026. The workshop examined scientific results, observational and data analysis challenges, extraction tools, and future opportunities. Discussions highlighted (1) the transformative potential of WFSS for the study of transient phenomena, galaxy evolution --both spatially-resolved and within the broader context of the cosmic web--, and rare populations and (2) the synergies among Euclid and Roman surveys, Rubin-LSST monitoring, JWST WFSS, and high-resolution integral-field observations. Participants identified advances in forward modeling and physics-informed machine learning as essential for addressing spectral overlap, crowded fields, and upcoming, very large data volumes. Realizing WFSS's full potential will require community-wide infrastructure, science-ready data products, accessible cloud-based analysis tools, and robust benchmarking of reduction pipelines. Crucially, participants called for systemic changes to properly recognize early-career researchers who invest significant efforts in pipeline, code, and calibration developments that enable WFSS science, and stressed that progress requires collaborative, multidisciplinary practices that optimize the participation and benefits of the next generation.

</details>


### [128] [The assembly of bulge-dominated galaxies: two evolutionary channels traced through morphology, kinematics, and environment with a hybrid classification pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23246)
*R. Flores-Freitas,R. R. de Carvalho,K. V. Nedkova,I. Ferreras,I. Kolesnikov,V. M. Sampaio*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过多维度观测数据（形态学、结构关系、恒星运动学、环境趋势）研究了核球主导星系的双峰分布物理起源，发现两类核球主导星系（G1高sSFR、G2低sSFR）在形态、亮度-尺度关系、角动量和环境分布上存在系统性差异，表明其具有不同形成历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Sampaio等人（2025）发现的核球主导星系双峰分布的物理成因。

Method: 结合非参数形态指标、结构标度关系、恒星运动学及环境趋势，在红移0.2–2.4范围内，利用基于MEGG的混合分类流程分析CANDELS成像，并交叉匹配MUSE光谱数据。

Result: G1星系形态与动力学介于盘星系与G2椭球星系之间；Kormendy关系零点存在跨红移一致偏移，G2中心面亮度更高；G1角动量更高、更似盘，G2更弥散主导；高质星系中G2比例随红移增长，而G1为稳定低质量群体；星系团中G1更倾向分布于大半径处。

Conclusion: G1与G2代表两种不同组装历史：G1源于缓慢的核球内禀增长（secular growth），G2则经历更剧烈的并合或快速淬灭过程；高密度环境加剧该双峰性。

Abstract: We investigate the physical origin of the bimodality in bulge-dominated galaxies, originally identified by Sampaio et al. (2025), by combining non-parametric morphological metrics, structural scaling relations, stellar kinematics, and environmental trends across a wide redshift range ($0.2 < z < 2.4$). Using the MEGG-based hybrid classification pipeline applied to CANDELS imaging, we examine the distributions of morphological metrics for two families of bulge-dominated galaxies: G1, with high specific star formation rate (sSFR) distributions, similar to discs, and G2 with lower sSFR. We find that G1 galaxies occupy an intermediate position between discs and G2 spheroids in morphological metrics, and this behaviour persists up to $z = 1.4$. Fitting the Kormendy relation separately for each family, we find a persistent offset in the zero-point across all redshifts: G2 galaxies are systematically brighter in mean effective surface brightness at fixed effective radius, likely indicating higher central stellar densities. This offset is present in both observed and rest-frame magnitudes, and we argue that it reflects a genuine difference in assembly history. A cross-match with MUSE observations reveals that G1 galaxies have higher projected angular momentum than G2 galaxies, with G1 galaxies overlapping with the disc population, while G2 galaxies are more dispersion-dominated. The redshift evolution of morphological fractions shows that, at high stellar masses, the G2 fraction grows, while discs follow the opposite trend. In parallel, G1 remains a stable, lower-mass population consistent with secular bulge growth. Finally, within galaxy clusters, G1 galaxies are preferentially found at larger cluster-centric radii, suggesting that high-density environments amplify the bimodality by accelerating quenching.

</details>


### [129] [Tracing Interstellar Gas in Quiescent Galaxies Using Na I D and Ca II H&K Absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23288)
*Arian Moghni,Timothy M. Heckman,Namrata Roy,Kyle B. Westfall,Kevin Bundy,Kate H. R. Rubin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用MaNGA IFU光谱数据，比较了Na I D和Ca II H&K吸收线在红喷泉星系及宁静星系中的分布与动力学特性，发现二者示踪不同气体组分：Na I D受尘埃屏蔽影响显著，主要存在于尘埃区；而Ca II因电离势更高，可延伸至更广区域，对尘埃依赖较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Na I D长期作为中性冷气体的光学示踪剂，而Ca II H&K（可示踪中性和电离气体）却未被充分研究；需厘清二者所揭示的气体物理性质及空间分布差异，以更全面理解星系气体流。

Method: 基于MaNGA积分视场光谱数据，对140个红喷泉星系及其140个宁静对照样本进行空间分辨分析；通过高斯拟合提取Na I D和Ca II K线的视向速度与线宽，并对比其空间延展性、面积、与尘埃分布的相关性。

Result: Ca II气体区域比Na I D大~6倍、径向延展约2倍、线宽宽1.7–1.8倍；Na I D与尘埃尺寸强相关，而Ca II无此相关；仅~20%星系中Na I D超出尘埃区，Ca II则达~70%。

Conclusion: Na I D与Ca II示踪截然不同的气体成分：前者高度依赖尘埃屏蔽以抵御电离辐射，后者因更高电离势而可在低尘埃区存活；联合使用二者可更完整刻画星系气体运动与分布。

Abstract: For over two decades, Na I D ($λ\lambda5891.58, 5897.56$ Å) absorption has been the primary optical tracer of cool, neutral gas in galaxies. In contrast, Ca II H&K ($λλ$3934.78, 3969.59 Å), which traces both neutral and ionized gas, has been comparatively unexplored. Here, we present a comparative study of the Na I D and Ca II H&K interstellar absorption lines using spatially resolved MaNGA IFU spectroscopy of 140 red geyser galaxies (local quiescent systems hosting low-luminosity AGN with bisymmetric ionized outflows) and a control sample of 140 quiescent galaxies. Using Gaussian fits to the Na I D and Ca II K lines, we measure gas velocity and dispersion. We find that the Ca II reservoirs are up to ~ 6 times larger in area, twice as radially extended, and have ~ 1.7 - 1.8x broader line widths than Na I D. Furthermore, Na I D shows a direct dependence on dust reservoir size, but the correlation vanishes for Ca II. In only ~ 20% of galaxies do the Na I D detections extend beyond dust regions, but this fraction jumps to ~70% for Ca II. Using these findings, we argue that dust plays a significant role in shielding Na I D from ionizing radiation, whereas Ca II, with its higher ionization potential, is not as dependent on shielding and can more easily survive beyond regions of dust. Na I D and Ca II trace distinct gas components, and studying both together provides a more complete picture of gas flows in galaxies.

</details>


### [130] [The CAMELS-CROCODILE Simulation Suite: A New Cosmology--Astrophysics Playground for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23982)
*Kentaro Nagamine,Yuri Oku,Atsushi J. Nishizawa,Jun-Young Lee,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Shy Genel,Daniel Anglés-Alcázar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CAMELS-CROCODILE模拟套件，它基于GADGET代码和大阪反馈模型，扩展了CAMELS框架，为机器学习推断提供了更广泛的亚网格物理模型覆盖；与IllustrisTNG相比，其标准模型表现出较弱的AGN反馈效应，并揭示了跨不同星系形成模型训练ML模型的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为拓展机器学习推断在不同星系形成物理模型上的鲁棒性，需构建独立于IllustrisTNG的、具有不同亚网格物理实现（如大阪反馈模型）的宇宙学流体模拟套件。

Method: 构建CAMELS-CROCODILE模拟套件，采用GADGET SPH代码和大阪反馈模型，在25Mpc/h与50Mpc/h盒子中系统变化26个宇宙学与天体物理参数；通过功率谱、恒星形成效率、Lyα吸收等观测量验证模型；并用在IllustrisTNG上预训练的图神经网络对其测试。

Result: CAMELS-CROCODILE标准模型对物质功率谱抑制仅数个百分点（对比IllustrisTNG的~27%），在群质量尺度晕中恒星形成效率高至2.5倍；GNN在跨模型推断Ω_m和σ_8时失败且后验过度自信，仅在训练分布内样本上误差为1.5倍。

Conclusion: 单一物理模型训练的ML推断器难以泛化至其他星系形成模型；未来需在多样化的物理实现上联合训练与测试ML模型，以提升宇宙学参数推断的可靠性。

Abstract: We present CAMELS-CROCODILE, a suite of cosmological hydrodynamic simulations that extends the CAMELS framework with the \gadget{} smoothed particle hydrodynamics code and the Osaka feedback model. As a companion to the IllustrisTNG-based second-generation CAMELS suite, it provides an independent implementation of the baryonic physics that shapes galaxies, the intergalactic medium, and large-scale structure, broadening the range of subgrid models over which machine-learning (ML) inference can be marginalized and tested. The suite comprises $25$ and $50Mpc/h$ boxes organized into one-parameter, cosmic-variance, and Sobol-sequence sets that vary $26$ cosmological and astrophysical parameters around a fiducial Osaka model. We describe the simulation design and validate the suite against benchmarks and other CAMELS suites. Compared with IllustrisTNG, the fiducial model suppresses the matter power spectrum by only a few per cent, against up to ${\sim}27\%$ at $z=0$, and forms stars with similar efficiency below $10^{12.2} M_\odot/h$ but up to $2.5$ times more efficiently in group-scale halos, both consistent with weaker AGN feedback. We also present the stellar and black hole bias and the Ly$α$ absorption around galaxies. As a first ML application, we apply a graph neural network trained on IllustrisTNG galaxy catalogs, with frozen weights, to CAMELS-CROCODILE. It fails to recover $Ω_{\rm m}$ and $σ_8$ and returns overconfident posteriors, mainly for simulations containing more galaxies than any in its training set; within that range $Ω_{\rm m}$ is recovered with an error only $1.5$ times the in-distribution value. These results underline the need to train and test ML inference across physically distinct galaxy formation models.

</details>


### [131] [Hierarchical Dynamics within Molecular Clouds: Type 4 Scaling Relations of Molecular Clouds in the Second Galactic Quadrant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23302)
*Suziye He,Yuehui Ma,Xuepeng Chen,Hongchi Wang,Miaomiao Zhang,Zhenyi Yue,Xiangyu Ou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用银河系成像卷轴绘画（MWISP）巡天的高灵敏度12CO和13CO J=1-0数据，对银河系第二象限分子云的层次结构进行统计分析，发现其动力学行为更受引力调控而非纯湍流支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分子云层次结构的动力学本质，检验湍流主导 vs. 引力主导模型在真实观测中的适用性。

Method: 基于MWISP巡天数据，采用DBSCAN与非二元Dendrogram算法识别2912个13CO云；分析Type 2（多云单示踪）与Type 4（单云单示踪）两类标度关系，并考察 linewidth-size、Keto-Heyer、virial parameter 等关键关系及其与臂结构、声速马赫数、密度等参数的关联。

Result: 发现：(1) linewidth-size指数随旋臂变化（Local Arm: ~0.32；Perseus/Outer Arms: ~0.42）；(2) Keto-Heyer关系在Local Arm中微弱，在其他臂中显著（斜率~0.3），且仅在体积密度形式下呈现清晰维里标度；(3) 维里参数与云尺寸、质量、面密度呈负相关；(4) 结构复杂度与面密度正相关，而非声速马赫数。

Conclusion: 观测结果整体更支持引力调控的层次动力学图像，而非纯湍流支撑模型。

Abstract: We present a statistical study of the hierarchical structure of molecular clouds in the second quadrant of the Galactic midplane (139.75 deg <= l <= 159.75 deg, -5.25 deg <= b <= 8.25 deg), based on high-sensitivity 12CO and 13CO J = 1-0 data from the Milky Way Imaging Scroll Painting survey. Using DBSCAN and a non-binary Dendrogram algorithm, we identify 2,912 13CO clouds. Type 2 (multicloud, single-tracer) scaling relations show arm-dependent trends: the power-law index of the linewidth-size relation is about 0.32 in the Local Arm and about 0.42 in the Perseus and Outer Arms. The classical Keto-Heyer relation is weak in the Local Arm but present in the Perseus and Outer Arms, with a slope of about 0.3. The virial parameter is anti-correlated with cloud size, mass, and surface density, consistent with previous surveys. Type 4 (single-cloud, single-tracer) scaling relations exhibit stronger internal correlations. The linewidth-size index spans about 0.3-0.8 and correlates positively with sonic Mach number, indicating that the relation is not universal but depends on cloud compressibility. While substructures do not follow the conventional surface-density-based Keto-Heyer relation, a volume-density formulation restores a clear virial scaling, with evolutionary tracks aligning along lines of constant virial parameter. Moreover, the structural complexity of molecular clouds, characterized by the relationship between the number of substructures and the min_delta parameter, correlates with surface density rather than sonic Mach number, contrary to results from turbulence-only magnetohydrodynamic simulations. Together, these results are more consistent with a gravity-regulated hierarchical dynamical picture than with a purely turbulence-supported scenario.

</details>


### [132] [A Sample of 459 Galaxy Groups Identified from the FAST All Sky HI Survey DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24060)
*Z. S. Yuan,Z. L. Wen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用FAST All Sky HI Survey (FASHI) DR2数据，首次直接基于中性氢（HI）信号识别出459个HI-rich星系群，发现HI总质量与恒星总质量呈正相关，但其比值随恒星质量增加而显著下降，表明星系群中存在渐进式HI耗尽现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建HI-rich星系群样本对研究星系演化及环境效应至关重要，而传统方法依赖光学预选，存在系统偏差；本文旨在利用当前最大最深的HI巡天数据，直接从HI信号出发识别星系群。

Method: 基于FASHI DR2 HI巡天数据，采用基于密度对比度判据和最小成员数（5个）的群组搜寻算法识别HI星系群；结合归档光谱数据证认光学对应体，并估算成员星系的恒星质量以表征群总质量。

Result: 获得包含3015个HI源的459个星系群样本；群红shift分布峰值在z~0.02，HI总质量峰值约10^{10} M_{\odot}；发现HI总质量与恒星总质量显著正相关，但M_HI/M_*比值随恒星总质量增加而显著下降。

Conclusion: HI-rich星系群中存在系统性的HI耗尽趋势，该样本为本地宇宙中星系演化与大尺度结构研究提供了丰富目标。

Abstract: Groups of HI-rich galaxies are key laboratories for understanding galaxy evolution and environmental effects, constructing samples of such groups is fundamental for relevant studies. Using the FAST All Sky HI Survey (FASHI) Data Release 2 (DR2), currently the largest and deepest HI catalog available, we identify galaxy groups directly from HI signals, without relying on optical pre-selection. A group-finding algorithm with a density contrast criterion and a minimum membership of five yields a catalog of 459 galaxy groups containing 3015 HI sources. Using archival spectroscopic data, we identify optical counterparts and derive total stellar masses of member galaxies as a proxy for the group total mass. The group redshift distribution peaks at z ~ 0.02, and the total HI mass peaks at ~ 10^{10} M_{\odot}. We find a significant positive correlation between total HI mass and total stellar mass, consistent with the increase in HI mass with halo mass reported in previous stacking studies. The ratio of total HI mass to total stellar mass decreases significantly with increasing total stellar mass, indicating gradual HI depletion in groups. Our sample provides rich targets for future studies of galaxy evolution and large-scale structure in the local Universe.

</details>


### [133] [Growth from KiDS: Stellar mass assembly of galaxies since z=2 in the light of Kilo-Degree Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23375)
*Qingshan Wang,Antonios Katsianis,Nicola R. Napolitano,Linghua Xie,Rui Li,Xiaohu Yang,Baitian Tang,Jiaxin Han,Zhenlin Tan,Lizhi Xie,Ying Tang,Yuchang Li,Hangxin Pu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用KiDS DR4数据测量了z=0-2红移范围内的星系恒星质量函数（GSMF），通过多种SED拟合方法分析系统误差，并探讨高质端异常及Eddington偏差对观测与理论模型对比的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究受限于观测体积，难以准确刻画高恒星质量端（log(M*/M⊙)>11.75）的星系质量函数；同时，系统误差（如SED拟合、红移估计、Eddington偏差）影响GSMF的可靠性，需系统评估。

Method: 基于KiDS DR4的2800万星系、9波段测光（u–Ks）和深度学习估算红移，采用5种SED拟合配置（CIGALE/LePhare与CB19/BC03/M05 SPS模型）；使用两种方法校正Eddington偏差，并以残差中值绝对偏差（MAD）为诊断指标对比半解析模型与流体模拟。

Result: 发现KiDS大天区覆盖使GSMF可延展至log(M*/M⊙)>11.75，暗示可能存在超出指数截断预期的更多高质星系；但Eddington偏差导致观测与模拟在高质端存在>0.5 dex不一致，提示理论模型参数调优需优先考虑EB效应。

Conclusion: 系统误差（代码、SPS模型、SFH假设）可达~0.6 dex；KiDS数据为高质端GSMF提供新约束，但需未来工作进一步确认异常特征；Eddington偏差是连接观测与理论的关键未充分处理因素，应前置纳入模型调优流程。

Abstract: We measure the galaxy stellar mass function at $z = 0-2$ using 28M galaxies from the KiDS DR4 which spans an effective survey area of 676.9 deg$^2$. Combining nine-band photometry ($u$--$K_s$) and redshifts derived using deep learning photometry, we use five SED fitting configurations, CIGALE/LePhare and CB19/BC03/M05 Stellar Population Synthesis (SPS). We present the impact of the systematics related to the code, SPS modeling and Star formation History (SFH) , which combined can introduce systematics of $\sim 0.6$ dex. The GSMFs outlined in this work agree well with previous studies (within the uncertainties introduced by systematics) that typically are able to cover measurements for the GSMF only up to $\log(M_*/M_\odot)\sim 11.75$, due to their limited volume. The large KiDS area and galaxy counts, allows us to gain some insights on the ``extreme" high-mass end, and our GSMFs imply that possibly there are more massive galaxies than we would expect, from a simple exponential cut-off, at $\log(M_*/M_\odot)>11.75$. However, we heed caution that despite advances done both in redshift estimation techniques and SPS, future studies are required to confirm this behavior. We consider the effects of Eddington-bias (EB) to our observations via two different methods and compare our results with the predictions of semi-analytic models and hydrodynamical simulations. We use the Mean Absolute Difference (MAD) of residuals as a diagnostic metric. Due to inconsistencies ($> 0.5$ dex) between observations and Eddington biased simulations found, especially at the high mass end, we suggest that some theoretical models would achieve better performance once parameter tuning takes into account the effects of EB prior to the tuning of the uncertain parameters involved related to stellar physics and feedback.

</details>


### [134] [StAGE: Stellar Archaeology-driven Galaxy Evolution II. Binary Black Hole Mergers in Quiescent Galaxies and their Star-forming Progenitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24222)
*Irene Iorio,Andrea Lapi,Lumen Boco,Giovanni Antinozzi,Michele Bosi,Cecilia Sgalletta,Mario Spera,Luigi Danese*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用StAGE框架结合星族合成模型，研究了宁静星系及其恒星形成前身中双黑洞并合率密度、质量分布及引力波背景，发现其对宇宙BBH并合率有显著贡献，但与LVK观测存在张力，尤其在高质端，并预测的随机引力波背景接近未来探测器灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宁静星系及其前身中双黑洞（BBH）的形成与并合历史，评估其对宇宙BBH并合率和随机引力波背景的贡献，并检验当前恒星演化与星系演化模型与LVK观测的一致性。

Method: 将StAGE（基于恒星考古的数据驱动星系演化框架）的恒星形成与化学增丰历史，与SEVN二元星族合成模型结果耦合；系统分析BBH并合率密度、质量分布、宿主星系性质及随机引力波背景；评估恒星考古假设、公共包层演化和α增强丰度等不确定性影响。

Result: QGs及其前身可贡献显著宇宙BBH并合率；多数BBH形成于Z≲Z⊙/3金属丰度环境，合并时宿主星系恒星质量≲10¹¹M⊙；z≳2时多位于主序，低红移时星暴与淬灭星系贡献上升；孤立双星可解释LVK观测的主质量分布，但在~40–50M⊙以上快速下降；动力学形成可填充高质端但加剧与LVK并合率的张力；预测的随机GW背景接近LVK升级及爱因斯坦望远镜灵敏度。

Conclusion: 当前基于孤立双星演化与星系演化耦合的模型虽能部分解释LVK观测，但在高质量BBH和本地并合率上存在明显张力，需重新审视恒星演化（如公共包层、金属丰度）与星系演化建模假设；动力学通道或为高质BBH重要来源，但需调整星团归一化以协调总并合率；预测的随机引力波背景具有可观测前景。

Abstract: We apply StAGE, a data-driven galaxy-evolution framework based on stellar archaeology, to study binary black hole (BBH) mergers in quiescent galaxies (QGs) and their star-forming progenitors. We combine StAGE star-formation and chemical-enrichment histories with pre-computed SEVN binary population-synthesis catalogs to derive the BBH merger-rate density, its dependence on intrinsic binary and host-galaxy properties, and the associated stochastic gravitational-wave (GW) background. We assess uncertainties related to stellar-archaeology prescriptions, common-envelope evolution, and $α$-enhanced abundances. We find that QGs and their progenitors can contribute a sizeable fraction of the cosmic BBH merger rate. For most of the binary-evolution prescriptions explored here, we highlight a tension with the local LVK-inferred merger rates, underscoring the need to revisit some assumptions in the underlying modeling. Most BBHs form at moderately subsolar metallicities, $Z\lesssim Z_\odot/3$, while their merger-time hosts typically have stellar masses $M_\star\lesssim10^{11}\,M_\odot$. At $z\gtrsim2$, hosts lie mainly on the galaxy main sequence, whereas starbursting and quenching descendants contribute increasingly toward lower redshift. Isolated binaries broadly reproduce the observed bulk of the primary- and chirp-mass distributions, but the predictions decline rapidly above $\sim 40-50\,M_\odot$, providing little support for the highest-mass systems inferred by LVK. Dynamical formation in young and globular clusters can populate this regime, but also increases the total merger-rate density, exacerbating the tension with LVK for the fiducial cluster normalizations adopted here. Finally, the predicted stochastic background approaches the projected sensitivity of planned LVK upgrades and lies within reach of the Einstein Telescope.

</details>


### [135] [On the Detectability and Measurement of Galactic Bars as a Function of Redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23501)
*Yi-Xiao Lyu,Luis C. Ho,Zhao-Yu Li,Ming-Yang Zhuang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种结合等光度椭圆拟合与傅里叶分解的自动化流程，利用本地棒旋星系图像模拟高红移观测，量化了观测偏差，并提出基于模拟优化的自适应判据和椭圆拟合法，显著提升高红移星系棒结构的探测率与测量可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移星系棒结构研究受限于分辨率下降和面亮度变暗等观测效应，亟需量化这些偏差并建立稳健的探测与测量方法。

Method: 构建自动化管道，融合等光度椭圆拟合与傅里叶分解；利用高质量本地棒旋星系图像人工红移生成HST（z≈0.5–3）和JWST（z≈1–6）条件下的模拟观测样本进行基准测试；采用红移依赖的自适应判据优化探测性能。

Result: 标准本地判据在高红移下严重低估棒分数（HST在z>0.5时漏检>50%，JWST在z>2时类似）；自适应判据使HST（z≈3）和JWST（z≈6）探测率超60%；椭圆拟合法测量更可靠（系统偏差4%–36%，可校正），而傅里叶法在低信噪比下易发生灾难性失败。

Conclusion: 推荐采用基于模拟的自适应判据与椭圆拟合法的标准工作流，并提供定量校正因子与经验关系，以从原始观测中恢复真实的棒统计性质。

Abstract: The cosmic evolution of galactic bars offers key insights into the dynamical history of disk galaxies. However, studies of high-redshift bars are severely hampered by observational effects such as resolution degradation and surface brightness dimming. To quantify these biases and establish a robust methodology of bar detection and measurement for high-redshift galaxies, we develop an automated pipeline integrating isophotal ellipse fitting and Fourier decomposition. We benchmark its performance using a comprehensive set of mock observations generated by artificially redshifting high-quality images of local barred galaxies to match the background and resolution of HST ($z \approx 0.5-3$) and JWST ($z \approx 1-6$) observations. We find that applying standard bar detection criteria derived from local galaxies to high-redshift data results in a severe underestimation of the bar fraction, missing $>50%$ of bars at $z>0.5$ in HST imaging and a similar fraction at $z>2$ in JWST imaging. Using adaptive, redshift-dependent criteria optimized via mock data achieves recovery rates above $60%$ out to $z \approx 3$ for HST and $z \approx 6$ for JWST. The ellipse fitting method significantly outperforms Fourier decomposition in measurement reliability. While ellipse fitting systematically underestimates bar length by $\sim 4%$--$36%$ due to surface brightness dimming, this bias is consistent and correctable ($σ\approx 0.88$--$2.22$); in contrast, the Fourier method is prone to catastrophic failures ($σ\approx 2.40$--$6.14$) in the regime of low signal-to-noise ratio. We propose a standardized workflow that prioritizes mock-derived adaptive criteria and ellipse fitting, providing quantitative correction factors and empirical relations that enable future studies to recover intrinsic bar statistics from raw observations.

</details>


### [136] [LATED: JWST integral field spectroscopy of a galaxy caught in chemical infancy at $z=4.8$ behind Abell 2744](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24589)
*Mingyu Li,Roberto Maiolino,Zheng Cai,Hannah Übler,Boyuan Liu,Qiao Duan,Fuyan Bian,Sijia Cai,Francesco D'Eugenio,Eiichi Egami,Bjorn H. C. Emonts,Xiaohui Fan,Yuki Isobe,Lucy R. Ivey,Xihan Ji,Gareth C. Jones,Maria Koller,Xiaojing Lin,Christopher C. Lovell,Kimihiko Nakajima,Masami Ouchi,Robert G. Pascalau,J. Xavier Prochaska,Jan Scholtz,Fengwu Sun,Sandro Tacchella,Yunjing Wu,Fujiang Yu,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过JWST/NIRSpec光谱观测确认LATED-1是一个极低金属丰度（Z/Z⊙ ≈ 0.58%）的矮星系，是目前已知金属丰度最低的星系之一，为检验第三星族星（Pop III）是否能在z<5持续形成提供了关键目标；同时验证了LATED光度选择方法在识别极低金属丰度星系方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定第三星族星（Pop III）形成何时结束仍是未解问题，亟需发现和证认z<5时仍可能存有Pop III恒星的极低金属丰度星系。

Method: 利用JWST/NIRSpec PRISM积分视场光谱对由LATED光度选择法识别出的候选体LATED-1（z=4.80）进行观测，并结合强发射线比R3=[O III]λ5007/Hβ及JWST标定的低金属丰度校准关系推算其氧丰度。

Result: 探测到显著Lyα、Hβ、Hα发射线及弱[O III]双线，得到R3=0.59^{+0.27}_{-0.21}，推得log(O/H)=6.45^{+0.17}_{-0.19}（即Z/Z⊙≈0.58%），确认其为迄今最贫金属星系之一；其微弱引力透镜放大（μ=2.80）使本征性质对透镜模型不敏感。

Conclusion: LATED-1是处于最早化学增丰阶段的矮星系，为检验Pop III恒星能否延续至z<5提供了强有力候选体；该工作证实LATED等光度选择法可有效识别低于1%太阳金属丰度的Pop III星系候选体，适用于后续光谱证认。

Abstract: When Population III (Pop III) star formation ended remains an open question. LATED-1 is an intrinsically faint ($M_\mathrm{UV}=-16.15$) Ly$α$ emitter at $z=4.80$ revealed by VLT/MUSE behind the lensing cluster Abell 2744 ($z=0.308$). Before any spectroscopic metallicity constraints, it was identified as an extremely metal-poor or metal-free galaxy candidate from JWST imaging by LATED, our novel photometric selection framework. Here we present serendipitous JWST/NIRSpec PRISM integral-field spectroscopy of this target. The spectrum reveals Ly$α$, H$β$, and H$α$ at 7.0, 5.6, and 17.7$σ$, as well as tentative detections of [O III]$λ\lambda4959,5007$ at 2.9$σ$, and yields $R3=\mathrm{[O\,III]\lambda5007/Hβ}=0.59^{+0.27}_{-0.21}$, upholding the earlier LATED photometric prediction of $R3<1.54$ ($2σ$ limit). The canonical JWST-based strong-line calibration, extrapolated to low metallicity, implies $\log (O/H)=6.45^{+0.17}_{-0.19}$, or $Z/Z_\odot=0.58_{-0.20}^{+0.28}\%$, placing LATED-1 among the most metal-poor galaxies known. Its modest magnification ($μ=2.80$) leaves the intrinsic properties insensitive to the lens model. LATED-1 is therefore a dwarf galaxy caught in the earliest state of chemical enrichment, and a compelling target for testing whether Pop III star formation can persist to $z<5$. The result demonstrates that photometric selection, especially through the LATED methodology, can reach below one per cent solar metallicity to identify Pop III galaxy candidates for spectroscopic follow-up.

</details>


### [137] [EP-FXT observations of the cool-core cluster Abell 478 out to R200: Thermodynamic properties and azimuthal asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23631)
*J. X. Sun,Y. Chen,S. M. Jia,C. K. Li,J. Zhang,X. J. Yang,H. Yu,A. Liu,X. Y. Zheng,W. W. Cui,D. W. Han,H. S. Zhao,X. F. Zhao,J. J. Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用爱因斯坦探针后随X射线望远镜（EP-FXT）对冷核星系团Abell 478进行深度观测，分析其从核心至R200的气体分布与热力学性质，发现该星系团整体接近准静态平衡，但西南方向存在由气体振荡引起的局部动力学扰动，影响了热力学测量与流体静力学质量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冷核星系团Abell 478在大尺度（至R200）上的气体分布、热力学性质及方位不对称性，并评估局部动力学扰动对流体静力学质量估计的影响。

Method: 利用EP-FXT的深度X射线观测数据，提取表面亮度、温度、电子数密度、压强、熵和总质量等径向剖面；采用双β模型拟合表面亮度，NFW模型拟合流体静力学质量分布；结合二维温度分布识别局部非平衡结构（如冷锋候选体）。

Result: 获得R200 = 2082 ± 95 kpc、M200 = (1.12 ± 0.15) × 10^15 M⊙；发现西南象限在中大半径处温度更低、密度更高、熵更低，并存在冷锋候选体；二维温度分布支持气体振荡解释。

Conclusion: Abell 478整体处于近似流体静力学平衡，但存在局部动力学扰动（如气体振荡），表明即使外观规则的冷核星系团，其局部非平衡结构仍会影响热力学诊断和质量估计。

Abstract: We use deep observations from the Einstein Probe Follow-up X-ray Telescope (EP-FXT) to investigate the gas distribution and thermodynamic properties of the cool-core galaxy cluster Abell 478 (A478) from the center out to $R_{200}$, and to examine its azimuthal asymmetry and the influence of local dynamical disturbances on hydrostatic mass estimates. We derive the surface-brightness, temperature, electron-density, pressure, entropy, and total mass profiles. The surface-brightness distribution is well described by a double-$β$ model, while the temperature profile shows the characteristic cool-core behavior, with a cool center, a rise toward intermediate radii, and a gradual decline in the outskirts. The hydrostatic mass profile is well fitted by a Navarro-Frenk-White model, yielding $R_{200}=2082\pm95$ kpc and $M_{200}=(1.12\pm0.15)\times10^{15}\,M_\odot$, indicating that the cluster is close to quasi-static equilibrium on global scales. Despite the globally regular structure, clear azimuthal asymmetry is present. The SW sector shows lower temperatures, higher densities, and lower entropies at intermediate and large radii, together with signatures consistent with a cold-front candidate. Combined with the two-dimensional temperature distribution, these features suggest local dynamical disturbance, likely associated with gas sloshing. A478 is therefore globally relaxed but locally disturbed, demonstrating that nonequilibrium structures can still affect thermodynamic measurements and hydrostatic mass estimates even in an apparently regular cool-core cluster.

</details>


### [138] [Age and Mass Signatures in the Multiband Second-order Image Structure of Young Star Clusters in M31](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23741)
*Yuan Liang,Chao-Wei Tsai,Jingwen Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过Hessian矩阵分析M31中1249个星团的多波段图像，发现星团年龄与UV波段峰核对比度参数C_S呈显著负相关，质量与结构组织参数Q呈负相关，表明年龄和质量在半解析图像中留下可探测的二阶空间结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星团年龄和质量是否会在半解析光度图像的二阶空间结构中留下系统性印记。

Method: 利用Hessian矩阵分析PHAT/AP六波段图像，识别并量化称为'峰核'的基本发光结构参数C_S和Q，并与CMD测定的星团年龄（10–300 Myr）和质量（10^{2.2}–10^{4.5} M⊙）进行相关性统计分析。

Result: 在F336W波段，年龄与C_S呈强负相关（ρ = −0.61），质量与Q呈负相关（ρ = −0.44）；表明UV峰核对比度随年龄减小，而质量增加会改变多波段中可探测结构的丰度与空间组织。

Conclusion: 星团年龄和质量确实在半解析图像中产生可观测、有物理意义的二阶结构差异，为仅凭成像数据估算星团基本参数提供了新途径。

Abstract: The ages and masses of star clusters may leave systematic signatures in the second-order spatial structure of semi-resolved photometric images. To test this hypothesis, we analyze the images using the Hessian matrix and identify basic luminous structures, termed peak cores. Applying this analysis to six-band PHAT/AP images of 1249 M31 clusters with CMD-based ages of 10--300 Myr and masses of $10^{2.2}$--$10^{4.5}\,M_{\odot}$, we examine the correlations of age and mass with the peak-core structural parameters, $C_S$ and $Q$. Younger clusters show larger $C_S$ values in the ultraviolet bands, with the strongest age--$C_S$ anti-correlation in F336W ($ρ=-0.61$), whereas more massive clusters show smaller $Q$ values, most clearly in F336W ($ρ=-0.44$). These correlations suggest that the contrast of UV-bright peak cores decreases with age, whereas increasing mass alters the abundance and spatial organization of detectable structures across different bands.

</details>


### [139] [A Galactic-scale extreme-precision radial velocity survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23874)
*Sukanya Chakrabarti,Jack Wagner,Robert J. de Rosa,Henrique Reggiani,Jason T. Wright,Peter A. Craig,Jacob K. Luhn,Francesco Pepe,Paul Robertson,Xavier Dumuesque,Massimiliano Bonamente*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个银河系尺度的极端精密径向速度（EPRV）巡天，旨在测量银河系加速度并约束暗物质分布，使用VLT上的ESPRESSO光谱仪对近太阳区域的宁静次巨星进行长期观测，已实现约50 cm/s的RV精度，并筛选出低抖动“黄金样本”，同时发现若干潜在伴星系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量银河系尺度的引力加速度，进而约束银河系暗物质分布；为下一代极大望远镜EPRV巡天提供路径验证。

Method: 利用VLT/ESPRESSO光谱仪（2021–2025年）对距太阳半径1 kpc、垂直高度约2 kpc内的宁静次巨星开展长期EPRV观测；通过RV散射筛选低恒星抖动的‘黄金样本’；结合恒星活动指标与轨道拟合识别伴星并估算其质量与周期；测定恒星大气与基本参数。

Result: 实现了约50 cm/s的RV精度；筛选出可用于十年尺度银河加速度测量的‘黄金样本’；识别出若干棕矮星、行星或未知双星候选体，并给出部分初始质量与周期约束；提供了‘黄金样本’的光球层与基本恒星参数。

Conclusion: 该巡天是首个银河系尺度EPRV探测，验证了利用长期高精度RV测量直接绘制银河系引力加速度场的可行性，为未来极大望远镜相关研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We describe the first Galactic-scale extreme-precision radial velocity (EPRV) survey and outline our goals of measuring Galactic accelerations and deriving constraints on the dark matter distribution in our Galaxy. We are targeting quiet subgiant stars within a kiloparsec in radius of the Sun and about 2 kiloparsec in vertical height relative to the Galactic mid-plane. The observations described here were carried out using the Echelle SPectrograph for Rocky Exoplanets and Stable Spectroscopic Observations (ESPRESSO) at the Very Large Telescope (VLT), between 2021-2025. We began by operating at a typical RV precision of $\sim$ 70 cm/s to select quiet stars from stellar activity indicators, and are now operating at $\sim$ 50 cm/s. We selected stars with low stellar jitter (determined from their RV scatter as a function of epoch) with the goal that they yield a measurement of the Galactic acceleration on approximately a decade timescale. We refer to this as our ``gold" sample. We also identify stars with possible companions (either brown dwarfs or planetary companions) or hitherto unknown stellar binaries that we have culled from our survey. For a few cases, we present initial constraints on the companion mass and orbital period. We also present the photospheric and fundamental stellar parameters for our ``gold" sample of sub-giant stars. This first Galactic-scale EPRV survey provides a pathfinder for next-generation surveys with extremely large telescopes, opening the possibility of directly mapping the gravitational acceleration field across the Milky Way and potentially beyond.

</details>


### [140] [Galaxy sizes in the early Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23911)
*P. Cataldi,S. Pedrosa,L. J. Pellizza,D. Ceverino,L. A. Bignone*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用高分辨率的FirstLight模拟，初步探讨了红移z≳5时星系的尺寸特性，发现了一个与红移无关、呈V字形的恒星质量-尺寸关系，峰值出现在约10^8.5太阳质量处，该特征由该质量尺度上由强烈中心恒星爆发引发的星系收缩阶段所塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高红移（z ≳ 5）下星系尺寸的演化规律，理解早期宇宙中星系结构形成的物理机制。

Method: 采用高分辨率、聚焦式FirstLight数值模拟，分析z ≳ 5星系的恒星质量与有效半径关系，并追踪其个体演化路径。

Result: 发现一个红移无关、V字形（chevron-shaped）的恒星质量–尺寸关系，峰值位于M⋆ ≈ 10^{8.5} M⊙；该形状源于该质量附近由强中心星暴触发的星系收缩相。

Conclusion: 早期星系的尺寸演化受其内部恒星形成活动（尤其是中心星暴）主导，而非单纯由宇宙学环境决定；V字形质量–尺寸关系是星系自演化的重要印记。

Abstract: We present preliminary results of a study of galaxy sizes at redshift $z \gtrsim 5$ using the high-resolution, zoom-in \textsc{FirstLight} simulations. We obtain a redshift-independent, chevron-shaped stellar mass--size relation with a peak at $M^{\star} \approx 10^{8.5}~\mathrm{M}_\odot$. This relation is shaped by the individual evolution of galaxies, which undergo a phase of contraction at this mass scale triggered by a strong central starburst.

</details>


### [141] [Gas phase Elemental abundances in Molecular cloudS (GEMS) XII. First look into the para-to-ortho ratio of c-C3D2 towards prestellar cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24285)
*J. Ferrer Asensio,A. Fuente,E. Roueff,K. Furuya,B. Tercero,N. Sakai*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次观测并确定了双氘代环丙烯亚基（c-C3D2）的对位-邻位核自旋同分异构体比例，发现部分源中该比值显著偏离统计值（1），可能源于p-D2H+或D3+驱动的化学反应，为相关分子形成模型提供了关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称分子的核自旋同分异构体（正/仲）比值保留其形成环境信息，但c-C3D2的该比值此前从未被观测测定，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 利用GEMS大型巡天项目新数据及文献数据，针对7个金牛座分子云源探测c-C3D2的正、对位跃迁；采用RADEX程序结合专用碰撞速率系数进行辐射转移分析，计算柱密度并导出对位/正位比值。

Result: 首次获得c-C3D2的对位/正位比值：3个新源分别为1.5、1.3、1.0；4个文献源分别为0.4、0.5、0.6、1.4；其中4个源符合统计比（~1），3个源显著偏高。

Conclusion: c-C3D2的非统计正/对位比值反映了特定离子（p-D2H+/D3+）主导的形成路径，独立于H2和c-C3H2的正/对位比，为星际化学模型提供了不可替代的观测基准。

Abstract: Symmetric molecules with identical nuclei form distinct nuclear spin isomers (ortho and para) that cannot interconvert via radiative or inelastic transitions, preserving clues about formation conditions. In the interstellar medium, cyclopropenylidene (c-C3H2) and its doubly-deuterated isotopologue (c-C3D2) provide a prime laboratory for studying these ratios. While measured for the main species, spin ratios for c-C3D2 remain unavailable. We present new detections of ortho and para transitions of c-C3D2 toward three Taurus molecular cloud sources from the GEMS Large program, and revisit four literature cores to investigate the para-to-ortho ratio of c-C3D2 for the first time. Column densities were computed using RADEX with dedicated collision rate coefficients, yielding total c-C3D2 column densities of (2.5-5.3) x 10e11 cm-2 for the new sources. The derived para-to-ortho ratios are 1.5(+0.7-0.6) (B213-C16-1), 1.3(+1.3-0.7) (TMC1-C), and 1.0(+1.4-0.7) (TMC1-NH3, statistical). For revisited sources L1544, TMC-1C, TMC-2, and L1517B, ratios are 0.4(+0.1-0.1), 0.5(+0.1-0.1), 0.6(+0.2-0.1), and 1.4(+0.7-0.5), respectively. Overall, four sources show statistical ratios and three show higher non-statistical values. This is the first observational determination of the para-to-ortho ratio in a doubly deuterated molecule using a detailed radiative transfer tool. The elevated ratios in some sources likely stem from p-D2H+ or D3+ driven reactions, and are independent of H2 and c-C3H2 ortho-to-para ratios. These findings offer an essential benchmark for chemical models investigating c-C3D2 formation pathways.

</details>


### [142] [Gravity-driven Emergence of Multi-fractal Density Structure in the Orion A Integral Shaped Filament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24319)
*Mengke Zhao,Guang-Xing Li,Keping Qiu,Guangya Zeng*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过多尺度分解重建方法分析猎户座A中积分形状纤维（ISF）的密度结构，发现其局部密度标度指数κ_ρ在不同子区域呈系统性变化（从OMC-4/5的≈−1.7到OMC-1的≈−2.3），表明ISF具有多重分形密度标度特征，并将其归因于引力驱动的差异性坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子云常被描述为自相似结构，但空间平均方法会丢失局部密度标度变化信息；需更精细刻画同一纤维状结构内密度随尺度变化的异质性。

Method: 采用多尺度分解重建（Multiscale Decomposition Reconstruction）方法处理赫歇尔（Herschel）柱密度图，提取积分形状纤维（ISF）各子区域（OMC-4/5、OMC-2/3、OMC-1）的局部密度标度指数κ_ρ（ρ ∝ r^{κ_ρ}），并结合像素级密度分布与气体纵向运动分析。

Result: ISF不同子区域κ_ρ系统性变陡：OMC-4/5 ≈ −1.7，OMC-2/3 ≈ −2.1，OMC-1 ≈ −2.3；像素级密度分布同步呈现更高体密度和更负κ_ρ；纵向气体流向OMC-1可能限制外区大尺度增长，维持κ_ρ梯度。

Conclusion: ISF并非单一标度的自相似结构，而是表现出多重分形密度标度；κ_ρ的空间梯度反映了引力主导的局地差异性坍缩过程，将局部密度标度与单一体系内的引力聚集直接关联。

Abstract: Molecular clouds are often described as self-similar structures, although spatially averaged measures do not retain local variations in density scaling. We use the density exponent $κ_ρ$ ($ρ\propto r^{κ_ρ}$) to characterize the density structure of the Integral Shaped Filament (ISF) in Orion\,A. Applying the Multiscale Decomposition Reconstruction method to the Herschel column-density map, we find distinct density--scale relations across the connected filament. Their slopes steepen from $κ_ρ\approx -1.7$ to $-1.9$ in the quiescent OMC-4/5 regions, through $\approx -2.1$ in the star-forming OMC-2/3, to $\approx -2.3$ in OMC-1, which hosts massive star formation. The ISF therefore does not follow a single local density-scaling exponent but exhibits multi-fractal density scaling. The pixel-level distributions show the same progression toward higher volume density and more negative $κ_ρ$. Since $κ_ρ$ measures the concentration of gas toward smaller scales, we interpret this sequence as gravity-driven differential collapse: denser regions have shorter free-fall times and develop steeper density profiles. Longitudinal gas motions toward OMC-1 may limit the mass supply available for large-scale growth in the outer sub-regions and help maintain the observed range of local exponents. These results link local density scaling to gravitational concentration within a single connected filamentary system.

</details>


### [143] [Physical conditions in PDRs revealed by IGRINS ro-vibrational H2 observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24630)
*Aurélien Piluso,Vincent Maillard,Raphael Meshaka,Emeric Bron,Franck Le Petit,Kyle Kaplan,Pierre Palud,Emilie Habart,Evelyne Roueff,Jacques Le Bourlot,Aristide Doussot,David Languignon,Nicolas Moreau*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用IGRINS光谱仪观测5个光解离区（PDRs）的H2高振转跃迁谱线，并结合Meudon PDR模型进行拟合，通过贝叶斯反演与MCMC方法约束物理条件，发现精确的紫外辐射场建模和最新的H2碰撞去激发率数据对成功再现观测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确理解光解离区（PDRs）中物理与化学过程，需借助高精度H2 ro-vibrational观测与先进模型联合分析，以约束关键物理参数如热压力和紫外辐射强度。

Method: 使用IGRINS光谱仪获取5个PDRs的高分辨率（R=45000）、通量定标且消光校正的1.45–2.45 μm H2谱线数据；采用Meudon PDR代码建模，输入基于Pollux数据库的恒星紫外谱，并结合χ²最小化与贝叶斯反演+MCMC后验采样进行参数拟合。

Result: 模型成功再现85%以上观测谱线（误差在2倍以内）；证实精确FUV辐射场建模与高振动能级H2碰撞去激发率是关键；H2谱线可有效约束热压力和FUV强度G0，但存在一定简并性；ALMA/JWST空间尺度信息有助于解除该简并；低激发PDR中观测支持尘埃表面形成的新生H2具有低转动、高振动能量的特征。

Conclusion: H2 ro-vibrational谱线是诊断PDR物理条件的有力探针，其解释必须依赖真实FUV辐射场与最新分子物理数据；多波段（尤其高空间分辨）观测与先进统计反演方法相结合，是突破参数简并、提升PDR物理认知的关键路径。

Abstract: We compare H2 high ro-vibrational observations of 5 PDRs (S140, IC63, Horsehead Nebula, NGC 2023 and Orion Bar) obtained with the IGRINS spectrograph to PDR models produced with the Meudon PDR code, in order to estimate the physical conditions and to constrain the physical and chemical processes that govern PDRs.
  We use newly flux-calibrated and extinction-corrected IGRINS H2 observations covering the 1.45-2.45 um range at a resolution of 45000, and adjust Meudon PDR models using both a simple chi2 minimization approach and a robust method using Bayesian inversion followed by posterior exploration via an advanced MCMC technique. The PDR models use specific incident FUV spectra for each PDRs based on theoretical stellar spectra from the Pollux database.
  The Meudon PDR code is able to reproduce more than 85% of the observed H2 ro-vibrational line intensities within a factor of two. We find that (1) a realistic modeling of the incident FUV field (both in term of geometry and of spectral shape) and (2) the inclusion of recent data on collisional de-excitation rates for high vibrational levels of H2 are key to this success. H2 ro-vibrational emission lines are found to provide good constraints on both the thermal pressure and the incident FUV field strength, G0, although with a non-negligible remaining degeneracy in most PDRs. Additional constraints, such as the spatial scales of the PDR derived from ALMA or JWST observations, are found to be able to lift this degeneracy. For low excitation PDRs, the observations provide evidence that nascent H2 molecules formed on grains have relatively low rotational energy and high vibrational energy, as predicted by theoretical and experimental studies of the surface formation of H2.

</details>

*B. Bidaran,A. Jiménez,R. García-Benito,M. Argudo-Fernández,M. Alcázar-Laynez,S. B. De Daniloff,S. Duarte Puertas,D. Espada,E. Florido,Y. K. González-Koda,I. Pérez,T. Ruiz-Lara,L. Sánchez-Menguiano,G. Torres-Ríos,P. Vásquez-Bustos,S. Verley,A. Zurita,M. A. Canossa-Gosteinski,A. M. Conrado,H. M. Courtois,I. del Moral-Castro,J. Falcón-Barroso,A. Ferré-Mateu,M. Hernández-Sánchez,A. Lugo-Aranda,S. Subramanian,P. Villalba-González,L. Galbany,R. M. González Delgado,R. F. Peletier,S. F. Sánchez,R. van de Weygaert,A. Fernández-Martín*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CAVITY巡天的第二版数据发布（DR2），提供了200个光学积分视场光谱（IFS）数据立方体，用于研究宇宙空洞中星系的演化特性及其与大尺度环境的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过研究宇宙空洞中星系的演化，分离大尺度环境对星系质量增长、物理性质调控及恒星形成向宁静态转变的影响。

Method: 利用Calar Alto 3.5米望远镜上的PMAS/PPAK光谱仪（V500配置）获取光学IFS数据，覆盖3745–7500 Å波段，分辨率为6 Å（FWHM）；采用统一选择标准，将样本数量较DR1翻倍至200个星系，并完成数据处理、质量控制与环境表征。

Result: 发布了200个高质量IFS数据立方体及配套星表，涵盖不同恒星质量、形态、颜色和电离状态的空洞星系；系统刻画了15个空洞的大尺度与局域环境多样性，包括大小、星系丰度、星系类型及局域结构（如星系群）。

Conclusion: CAVITY DR2显著增强了统计能力，为深入理解空洞星系演化及其环境依赖性提供了坚实的数据基础，并已公开发布供社区使用。

Abstract: Studying galaxy evolution under the unique environmental conditions of cosmic voids provides an opportunity to disentangle the role of the large-scale environment in shaping mass assembly (both baryonic and dark matter), regulating galaxy physical properties, and driving the transformation from star-forming to quiescent systems. We present the second public data release (DR2) of the Calar Alto Void Integral-field Treasury Survey (CAVITY), an ongoing legacy programme designed to study void galaxies (VGs) across a range of nearby ($0.005 \leq z \leq 0.050$) voids with different sizes and dynamical stages, providing 200 science-grade optical integral-field spectroscopy (IFS) data cubes. By doubling the number of galaxies relative to DR1 while maintaining the same selection criteria, DR2 significantly increases the dataset's statistical power, enabling more robust characterization of galaxy properties and their correlations with environment. The observations were obtained with the PMAS/PPAK spectrograph on the 3.5-m Calar Alto telescope using the V500 configuration, covering the optical spectral range of 3745-7500 A at a resolution of 6 A (FWHM). The DR2 VG sample spans a broad range of stellar masses, morphologies, colours, and gas ionisation properties. We describe the sample selection, observing strategy, data reduction pipeline, quality-control procedures, and the public access to the CAVITY datasets and associated ancillary data on the survey's database. In addition, we present a characterisation of the large- and local-scale environments of CAVITY galaxies, showing that the 15 surveyed voids encompass a wide variety of sizes, galaxy richness, galaxy populations, and local structures, including galaxy groups. This release comprises 200 IFS data cubes, publicly available together with the master catalogues at the survey's dedicated webpage: https://cavity.caha.es/data/dr2/.

</details>


### [145] [A Newly Discovered VVVX Star Cluster Surfing the Galactic Disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24811)
*Elisa R. Garro,José G. Fernández-Trincado,Casmir O. Obasi,Dante Minniti,Matías Gómez,Roberto K. Saito,Javier Alonso-García,Alonso Luna,Valentin D. Ivanov,Maria Gabriela Navarro,Sara Federle,Bernardo P. L. Pereira,Beatriz Barbuy,Devika Bhadrakumar Sindhu,Bruno Dias,Tali Palma,Ilaria Petralia,Sasi Saroon*

TL;DR: 本文报告了在银河系盘区发现的新恒星团Garro 04，通过VVVX测光与KMOS光谱观测确认其为真实星团，并测定其年龄约5 Gyr、金属丰度[Fe/H] = -0.5，属古老疏散星团或介于球状与疏散星团之间的特殊天体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并确认银河系盘区中此前未被识别的恒星团，拓展对银河系恒星系统多样性的认知。

Method: 结合VVVX巡天测光数据识别恒星过密区，并利用ESO甚大望远镜KMOS仪器进行K波段中分辨率光谱观测，联合测光与光谱分析确定其物理与运动学参数。

Result: 确认Garro 04是一个具有共同视向速度和化学丰度的致密恒星系统；测定其距离、结构参数、运动学及轨道特性，表明其始终束缚于银盘内；年龄为5±2 Gyr，金属丰度[Fe/H] = −0.5±0.2。

Conclusion: Garro 04是一个真实的古老星团，可能属于古老疏散星团，或处于经典球状星团与疏散星团分类边界的特殊天体，为理解银河系恒星团演化与分类提供新案例。

Abstract: We report the discovery of Garro 04, a previously unidentified stellar cluster toward the Galactic disc, uncovered as part of the ESO/KMOS VVVX-GalCen Spectroscopic Survey. This object was serendipitously confirmed as a stellar overdensity in the VISTA Variables in the Vía Láctea eXtended (VVVX) survey footprint and subsequently confirmed through medium-resolution $K$-band spectroscopy obtained with the K-band Multi Object Spectrograph (KMOS) at the ESO Very Large Telescope. The combined photometric and spectroscopic analysis reveals a coherent stellar population with common radial velocities and chemical properties, supporting its classification as a genuine star cluster rather than a statistical fluctuation of the disk field. We derive the fundamental properties of Garro 04, including its structural parameters, distance, kinematics, and compute its orbital properties, finding that its motion remains confined to the Galactic disk region. Finally, our analysis suggests that Garro~04 is likely an old open cluster, or an object straddling the boundary between the canonical globular and open cluster classifications, with an estimated Age $ = 5 \pm 2$ Gyr and a metallicity of $\mathrm{[Fe/H]} = -0.5 \pm 0.2$, finding an excellent agreement between the photometric and spectroscopic determinations.

</details>


### [146] [Reducing False Positives in Strong-Lens Searches with Generalized-Mean Consensus of Machine-Learning Ensembles in the Kilo-Degree Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24891)
*Ziqi Li,Rui Li,Xu Huang,Hui Li,Pufan Liu,Liang Gao,Crescenzo Tortora,Nicola N. Napolitano,Xiaoyue Cao,Ran Li,Liqing Chen,Kang Jiao,Valerio Busillo,Yue Dong*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义均值共识的多模型集成方法，显著降低了KiDS DR4数据中强引力透镜候选体的误报率，大幅减少了人工目视检查工作量，同时保持高完备率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽视场巡天中强透镜搜寻面临的主要挑战是海量假阳性样本，导致后续人工检验和构建统计上有意义的透镜样本成为瓶颈。

Method: 训练了多种深度学习模型（包括卷积网络、Transformer及其变体、混合模型），并采用算术平均与广义均值对各模型输出概率进行集成融合。

Result: 在真实KiDS DR4数据上，广义均值集成将90%完备率下的误报率从单模型的0.016–0.020降至0.007；对LRG和BG样本分别减少约50%和70%候选数；最终获得170个高质量新候选体（24个A类、146个B类）及1706个C类候选体。

Conclusion: 广义均值共识的模型集成策略是一种实用有效的方法，可在保持高回收率的同时显著降低视觉检验负担。

Abstract: Context. In wide-field surveys, the main challenge is not just classifier sensitivity, but the overwhelming number of false positives. Searching for strong lenses among millions to bilions of galaxies produces many contaminants, making the bottleneck for follow-up inspection and building statistically useful lens samples. Aims. We aim to improve the purity of strong-lens candidate selection in KiDS DR4 by combining several classifiers. The objective is to retain high completeness for known candidates while substantially reducing the fraction of non-lenses. Methods. We trained convolutional, Transformer-based, and hybrid classifiers, including Li ResNet+, Swin Transformer variants, Swin-MLP, and DemiLensNet. Their probabilistic outputs were combined at score level using averaging and a generalized mean consensus. The models were tested on simulated KiDS-like lens images and then evaluated on real KiDS DR4 lens candidates embedded in a non-lens sample. Results. On the simulated test set, ensembles show no advantage over the best single models. On the mixed real KiDS test set, the arithmetic mean reduces the false-positive rate at 90% completeness from 0.016-0.020 (the range spanned by the two best individual models) to 0.011 for the seven-model ensemble. The generalized mean reduces it further, to 0.007. Applied to the full LRG and BG samples at the same 90% completeness level, the generalized mean reduces returned candidates by roughly 50% for LRGs and 70% for BGs, relative to the best single model. After visual inspection, we obtain 170 new high-quality candidates (24 Class A and 146 Class B), together with 1706 Class C candidates. Conclusions. Our results demonstrate that the generalized mean consensus of an ML ensemble strategy provides a practical route to reducing the visual inspection workload while preserving a high recovery rate of promising strong-lens candidates.

</details>


### [147] [Galaxy evolution in the post-merger regime. V - Atomic gas evolution traced by ALFALFA stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24925)
*Sara L. Ellison,Manasvee Saraf,Leonardo Ferreira,Amelie Saintonge,Dirk Scholte,Jing Wang,Qifeng Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MUMMI机器视觉流水线，利用DECaLS成像识别并预测星系并合后的时间（T_PM），构建了包含19951个并合后星系的MUMMI-DECaLS星表；通过与ALFALFA巡天交叉匹配，发现并合后星系整体HI匮乏，并揭示其HI含量演化强烈依赖于并合后时间及星系恒星形成状态：恒星形成型并合后星系在并合初期HI略富集，中期显著亏损，约1.5 Gyr后恢复；而宁静型并合后星系则始终HI匮乏，但仍保留可观HI质量（>10^9 M_sun），可能在未来重启恒星形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解原子氢（HI）气体在星系并合生命周期中的作用，尤其是HI含量如何随并合后时间及星系恒星形成状态演化，以解决不同研究间结果不一致的问题。

Method: 扩展MUMMI机器视觉流水线至DECaLS成像数据，构建MUMMI-DECaLS并合后星系星表（含19951个样本，其中14638个有稳健的T_PM预测）；将其与ALFALFA 21cm HI巡天交叉匹配，对752个并合后星系及其控制样本进行中位谱堆叠分析。

Result: 并合后星系整体比非相互作用星系HI匮乏0.11 dex；HI演化强烈依赖T_PM和星系类型：恒星形成型星系在并合后立即出现0.1 dex HI富集，0.16–0.96 Gyr内达0.3 dex亏损，~1.5 Gyr后恢复正常；宁静型星系在所有T_PM阶段均HI匮乏，但仍有>10^9 M_sun HI残留。

Conclusion: 星系并合中HI行为高度复杂，不仅取决于并合时间，还受恒星形成状态调控；该双重依赖性解释了不同研究间的差异，提示未来需联合时序与星系分类开展HI演化研究。

Abstract: (Abridged) In this work, we investigate the role of atomic hydrogen (HI) gas in the galaxy merger lifecycle. In order to both identify galaxies in mergers, as well as to predict their time since coalescence, we present an extension of the MUlti Model Merger Identifier (MUMMI) machine vision pipeline applied to Dark Energy Camera Legacy Survey (DECaLS) imaging. The new MUMMI-DECaLS catalog contains 19,951 post-mergers, of which 14,638 have robust time post-merger (T_PM) predictions. The MUMMI-DECaLS post-merger galaxy catalog is cross-matched with the Arecibo Legacy Fast ALFA (ALFALFA) survey. For the 752 post-mergers thus identified, as well as a matched control sample, we produce median spectral stacks of 21~cm emission, finding that the post-mergers are HI deficient by 0.11 dex compared with non-interacting galaxies. When divided into T_PM bins we find that the HI deficit (or excess) is strongly dependent on both the time since coalescence and whether the galaxies are star-forming or quenched. Star-forming post-mergers in particular show a strong HI evolution with T_PM, exhibiting a 0.1 dex MHI excess immediately after coalescence, a 0.3 dex MHI deficit at 0.16 < T_PM < 0.96 Gyr and normal gas masses by ~ 1.5 Gyr after coalescence. On the other hand, despite being matched in star formation rate, stellar mass and redshift, quenched post-mergers are HI deficient at both short and long post-coalescence times. Nonetheless, a significant mass of HI (>10^9 M_sun) remains even in these late-time quenched post-mergers, representing a resource which could potentially re-fuel star formation in the future. Our results demonstrate the complexity of quantifying the HI behaviour in mergers, which depends not only on timing, but also star-forming status, which can complicate comparisons between different studies and samples.

</details>


### [148] [EPOCHS-DR2 I: Expanded data release and properties of $6.5<z<16.5$ photometrically selected galaxies across NIRCam deep fields covering $\sim700\,\rm arcmin^2$ with the galfind software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24987)
*Duncan Austin,Christopher J. Conselice,Thomas Harvey,Nathan J. Adams,Louis Quilley,Jordan C. J. D'Silva,Vadim Rusakov,Qiong Li,Lewi Westcott,Caio Goolsby,James Arcidiacono,Kai Madgwick,William J. Roper,Brenda Frye,Norman A. Grogin,Rafael Ortiz,Rogier Windhorst*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Epochs2样本，包含2452个红移6.5<z<16.5的恒星形成星系，基于JWST/NIRCam深场图像，经光度测量和红移验证，揭示高红移星系尘埃含量比预期高，并识别出一批高置信度z>10.5候选体及褐矮星污染源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探索宇宙再电离时期（z>6）特别是z>10的早期星系性质，需构建大样本、高可靠性高红移星系目录；同时需系统识别并剔除前景污染物（如褐矮星），提升样本纯度。

Method: 使用GalFind光度测量流程处理六个主流JWST/NIRCam深场数据（约650–740 arcmin²），结合光谱红移验证性能（σ_NMAD=0.017, η=4.3%），识别并剔除‘小红点’（LRDs）和褐矮星候选体；采用SED拟合（eazyPy、bagpipes、bdFinder）分析UV斜率β_UV与绝对星等M_UV关系，并定义‘gold sample’（z_phot>10.5）以评估高红移可靠性。

Result: 获得2452个高红移星系样本（其中293个光谱证认），发现β_UV–M_UV关系在z≈12.5趋于平缓，中位β_UV(−19)=−2.32，表明存在显著尘埃消光；识别出62个‘gold’样本（85%无NIRSpec跟进），含15个z_phot>13.5候选体；发现两个强候选体GS-W-4608和GN-Med-1881（β_UV≲−3，Lyα break >1.6 mag，F335M/F410M探测支持）。

Conclusion: 高红移（z>12）星系普遍存在比预期更蓝但非极端的UV斜率，暗示早期星系已含一定尘埃；Epochs2样本及其配套数据（PSF、SED拟合结果、多波段星表）构成JWST时代关键基准资源，支撑Euclid/Roman宽视场巡天的后续研究。

Abstract: We present the \epochstwo\ sample of $2452$ star forming galaxies at $6.5<z_{\rm phot}<16.5$ produced using the \galfind\ photometric pipeline across $\sim650-740~\mathrm{arcmin}^2$ in six of the most widely used deep, blank field \jwst/NIRCam imaging surveys. $293$ galaxies are spectroscopically confirmed and a spectroscopic--photometric redshift comparison yields a normalized median absolute deviation, $σ_{\rm NMAD}=0.017$, an $η=4.3\%$ outlier rate, and a mean offset $\langleΔz\rangle=-0.022$. We find $34$ little red dots (LRDs) with ``V--shaped'' SEDs, as well as $69$ L--type, $36$ T--type, and $2$ compact Y--type brown dwarf candidates, which we remove from our sample. The fiducial sample yields a flattening $β_{\rm UV}-M_{\rm UV}$ relation with only moderately blue median $β_{\rm UV}(M_{\rm UV}=-19)=-2.32^{+0.08}_{-0.07}$ at $z\simeq12.5$, implying dust-polluted stellar populations at the highest redshifts. A ``gold'' sample of $62$ sources are identified at $z_{\rm phot}>10.5$, $\sim85\%$ of which are without NIRSpec follow-up, including $15$ candidates at $z_{\rm phot}>13.5$ across a diverse range of proper sizes $30\,\rm pc\lesssim r_{\rm c}\lesssim1.5\,\rm kpc$ and UV slopes $-4.0\lesssimβ_{\rm UV}\lesssim-1.5$. Two very promising candidates stand out, GS-W-4608 and GN-Med-1881, with $β_{\rm UV}\lesssim-3$, $\gtrsim1.6\,\rm mag$ \lya\ break colors, and F335M and F410M detections which improve their high-redshift solution robustness. Complementary to our high-redshift sample, this public data release provides the community with optical/NIR catalogs, empirical PSFs, and \eazypy, \bagpipes, and \bdfinder\ SED fitting results, enhancing the legacy value of photometric JWST data in the upcoming era of wide-field imaging datasets with Euclid and Roman.

</details>

### [149] [Theory of Gravity as Theory of Local Inertial Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22354)
*Bartłomiej Bąk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对称仿射联络的广义引力理论，将引力解释为牛顿第一定律的局域化形式，并建立了仿射图像到度规图像的完整转换，揭示了非度规性在度规图像中可解释为物质场（如电磁场）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广义相对论以度规为基本对象，而本文旨在探索以对称仿射联络为基本变量的引力理论，寻求引力更本源的几何与物理诠释，并统一引力与其它基本相互作用（如电磁力）的几何描述。

Method: 采用仿射几何框架，以对称联络为基本动力学变量，构建基于全黎曼张量的引力理论；通过变分原理建立理论形式体系；重点研究仿射图像向度规图像的转换机制，特别是非度规性张量的物理含义。

Result: 证明了对称仿射联络理论构成广义引力的扩展理论；确立了引力作为局域惯性系场和牛顿第一定律局域化的自然起源；首次在全般性下完成从全黎曼张量理论到常规度规理论的转换；证实非度规性在度规图像中表现为物质场，复现并推广了Weyl对电磁场的几何解释。

Conclusion: 仿射理论是比度规理论更基本的引力描述框架；非度规性不仅是几何自由度，更是物质场的几何起源；该框架为引力与规范场的统一提供了新的几何路径。

Abstract: It is proved that the affine theory of the full Riemann tensor constitutes the extended theory of gravity. The fundamental object here is a spacetime symmetric connection. Its physical interpretation is that of a field of local inertial frames. In this approach, gravity arises, in a natural way, as a local version of Newton's First Law. A variational formulation of the theory is presented, based on the results introduced in the article co-authored by the present author and one of the supervisors. The general framework is supported by several examples. A central result of the dissertation is the transition from the affine picture to the metric picture. A simplified version of this procedure -- valid for a specific class of Lagrangians. It was proved there that the non-metricity in the affine picture can be interpreted as a matter field in the metric picture. This is precisely Hermann Weyl's interpretation of electromagnetism. Here, the transformation from the full Riemann tensor theory to the conventional metric theory is examined in full generality for the first time and illustrated using the previously introduced examples.

</details>


### [150] [Sourced Gravitational Radiation in Parity-Violating Tetrad Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22396)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含三个偶宇称挠率不变量和一个奇宇称耦合项的四参数四维理论中的线性引力模、远区场源及二次能量平衡，发现存在八种自由度（2张量+2标量+4矢量），其中矢量模哈密顿量非正定，尽管动能本征值为正且传播速度为实数；推导了所有极化模式的源-振幅关系，并证明了在所选一阶导数四维拉格朗日下，二次诺特定流等于二阶Møller流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含挠率的广义引力理论中线性激发、能量动量输运及宇称破缺效应，特别是矢量模的稳定性与物理可接受性问题。

Method: 围绕闵氏时空线性化四参数四维引力理论，采用标量-矢量-张量分解，分析自由度数目、传播速度、动能本征值与哈密顿符号性质；推导紧致源响应、诺特定流与Møller流的等价性，并计算各模态能量动量流。

Result: 发现8个线性自由度（2张量、2标量、4矢量）；张量与标量模以光速传播，矢量模传播速度依赖耦合参数；矢量哈密顿量非正定（即使动能正定且速度实）；获得全部8种极化的源-振幅解析关系；证明二次诺特定流等于二阶Møller流；矢量能量流仅为形式符号响应。

Conclusion: 该四参数挠率引力理论在线性水平上具有丰富模结构，但普遍存在矢量模哈密顿无下界问题，挑战其作为健康有效理论的物理自洽性；结果涵盖并推广了已有文献（如Shirafuji-Nashed 1997）的静态极限。

Abstract: We study linear gravitational modes, sourced far-zone fields, and quadratic energy balance in a four-parameter tetrad theory of gravity. The theory contains three parity-even torsion invariants and a parity-odd coupling $v^μa_μ$, where $v_μ$ and $a_μ$ are the vector and axial torsion components. Linearizing around Minkowski spacetime and applying a scalar--vector--tensor decomposition, we find two tensor, two scalar, and four vector degrees of freedom in the generic nondegenerate parity-violating region. The tensor and scalar modes propagate at the speed of light, while the two vector branches have coupling-dependent propagation speeds. We derive their kinetic eigenvalues, characteristic speeds, and flux normalizations. An exact trace identity shows that the vector Hamiltonian is not positive definite in the generic branch, even when the kinetic eigenvalues are positive and the propagation speeds are real. We also derive the response to a general compact canonical source and obtain explicit source-to-amplitude relations for all eight linear polarizations. The field $V_μ=\partial^νB_{νμ}$ is used to illustrate intrinsic-spin multipoles. {We further prove that the quadratic Noether current equals the second-order Møller current for the chosen first-derivative tetrad Lagrangian and derive the corresponding mode-resolved energy and momentum fluxes.} The vector contribution remains a formal signed response because of the unbounded Hamiltonian. The stationary limit reproduces the results of Ref.~\cite{ShirafujiNashed1997}, and a representative parameter scan illustrates vector-mode splitting and kinetic degeneracy.

</details>


### [151] [Charged particles and fields in bumblebee gravity: Periodic orbits and superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22482)
*Marco A. A. de Paula,Renan B. Magalhães,V. B. Bezerra,A. A. Araújo Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了bumblebee引力理论中带电黑洞背景下带电粒子和场的动力学，重点分析了洛伦兹破缺参数l对内稳定圆轨道、吸收截面及超辐射现象的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解洛伦兹破缺参数l在bumblebee引力中对带电黑洞物理行为（如粒子轨道、波吸收与超辐射）的影响。

Method: 推导带电bumblebee黑洞解；分析其几何与物理性质；计算带电粒子的圆轨道与束缚轨道；数值计算标量波吸收截面，并结合修正的渐近动量与通量；在低频与高频极限下给出解析近似。

Result: 内稳定圆轨道半径随l增大而增大；吸收截面存在三种区域（无界吸收、有界吸收、有界超辐射）；超辐射阈值为ω_c，且超辐射放大率随l增大而减小；l<0时超辐射强于RN黑洞；吸收截面因几何因子1/(1+l)随l增大而减小。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破缺参数l显著影响黑洞周围粒子运动与波传播行为，尤其调控超辐射强度与吸收特性，为检验洛伦兹对称性破缺提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles and fields in the background of a charged black hole in bumblebee gravity, aiming to better understand the role played by the Lorentz-violating parameter $l$. We first review the derivation of the charged bumblebee black hole and investigate its main physical and geometrical properties. We then obtain the circular and bound trajectories of charged particles and find that the innermost stable circular orbit increases with $l$. By using a numerical approach, we compute the total absorption cross section for arbitrary values of the scalar wave frequency $ω$. Since the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we construct the total absorption cross section by taking into account the modified asymptotic momentum and flux. Analytical approximations are derived in the low- and high-frequency regimes. The low-frequency expression displays a transition velocity that separates two distinct asymptotic branches and generalizes the corresponding Reissner-Nordström result. Our numerical results for the cross section agree with the analytical approximations in their corresponding limits, and the absorption parameter space can be divided into three regions: Unbounded absorption, bounded absorption, and bounded superradiance. In particular, superradiance occurs whenever $ω<ω_{c}$, where $ω_{c}$ is the threshold for superradiance. Moreover, the superradiance amplification decreases as we increase $l$, and charged bumblebee black holes with $l < 0$ lead to greater superradiant scattering than in the Reissner-Nordström case. We also note that although increasing $l$ lowers the potential barrier, it reduces the total absorption cross section because of the geometrical factor $1/(1+l)$ present in the cross section formula.

</details>


### [152] [Flexible numerical characteristic gluing to extremal Reissner-Nordström black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22518)
*Junwoo Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用数值方法研究了通过特征线粘接法构造极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞的引力坍缩解，改进了无质量标量场下粘接可行的黑洞尺寸下界，并为有质量标量场的质量-电荷比临界值（等于1）提供了强数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极端黑洞形成过程中第三定律的可能违背，特别是通过特征线粘接法构造相关解的可行性边界。

Method: 采用数值方法分析特征线粘接构造，分别针对无质量与有质量的带电标量场引力坍缩模型。

Result: 1) 显著提升了无质量标量场情形下可成功粘接的最终黑洞尺寸的下界；2) 在有质量标量场情形下，为质量-电荷比临界值等于1的理论预言提供了强有力的数值支持。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持极端黑洞可通过特定参数范围内的标量场坍缩实现，并验证了质量-电荷比临界阈值的精确性，强化了对黑洞力学第三定律边界的理解。

Abstract: Recent work has used the method of characteristic gluing to construct spacetimes violating the third law of black hole mechanics. This paper uses numerical methods to investigate two aspects of the gluing construction of solutions describing the gravitational collapse of a charged scalar field to form an extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. First, for a massless scalar field, we improve significantly the lower bounds on the size of the final black hole for which gluing is possible. Second, for a massive scalar field, it is known that the formation of an extremal Reissner--Nordström black hole is impossible if the mass-to-charge ratio of the scalar field exceeds unity. We present strong evidence that this bound is sharp by constructing gluing solutions with a scalar field mass-to-charge ratio just below unity.

</details>


### [153] [Black Holes as Frequency-Dependent Filters of Stochastic Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22644)
*Sefi Katznelson,Aidan Minger,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文定量分析了黑洞对引力波背景的响应，指出其作为频率、角度和偏振相关滤波器的作用；对于施瓦西黑洞，各向同性背景下的弹性散射不产生净单极信号，仅视界吸收导致谱形畸变；计算了该传输函数并关联到因果环降响应；推广至宇宙学黑洞群体后发现光学深度可忽略；对于克尔黑洞，尽管存在超辐射放大，但各向同性入射与随机自旋取向显著稀释了弥散信号，因此可观测效应更可能来自罕见、邻近、自旋对齐、高速旋转或瞬时被照亮的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清黑洞在受扰动（产生准正规模）与处于稳态引力波背景（散射问题）下的不同物理行为，并定量刻画其作为滤波器对引力波背景的响应。

Method: 将黑洞建模为频率、角度和偏振依赖的滤波器；解析计算施瓦西黑洞对各向同性稳态引力波背景的吸收传输函数；建立其与因果环降响应的联系；推广至宇宙学黑洞群体的角-偏振输运核；扩展至克尔黑洞，考虑超辐射及自旋取向统计。

Result: 施瓦西黑洞在各向同性背景下无净单极散射信号，仅吸收造成谱畸变；宇宙学尺度下光学深度可忽略（包括作为全部暗物质的原初黑洞）；克尔黑洞虽有超辐射放大，但因入射各向同性与自旋随机，弥散信号被强烈稀释。

Conclusion: 黑洞对稳态引力波背景的响应本质上是散射/吸收问题，而非激发新准正规模；可观测效应极难通过宇宙学累积获得，而更可能出现在特殊、局部、强耦合的黑洞系统中。

Abstract: Black holes ring when perturbed, whereas their response to a stationary gravitational-wave background is a real-frequency scattering problem, not a source of additional quasi-normal-mode lines. We make this standard distinction quantitative by treating a black hole as a frequency-, angle-, and polarization-dependent filter. For an isotropic stationary background around Schwarzschild holes, elastic scattering produces no net monopole signal, so horizon absorption is the only population-level spectral distortion. We calculate this transfer function for Schwarzschild holes in detail, identify its absorptive and phase-delay signatures, and connect the stationary response to the causal ringdown excited by a finite wave packet. We then promote the single-hole result to an angular and polarization transport kernel for a cosmological population. The resulting optical depth is negligible for realistic black-hole populations, including asteroid-mass primordial black holes comprising all dark matter. We then extend the analysis to Kerr holes, for which superradiance allows genuine amplification in selected co-rotating modes, but isotropic incidence and random spin orientations strongly dilute the diffuse signal. Observable effects are therefore more likely in rare, nearby, aligned, rapidly spinning, or transiently illuminated systems than through cumulative cosmological propagation.

</details>


### [154] [An exact Kerr-like rotating Morris--Thorne wormhole: throat, ergoregion and equatorial shadow slice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22823)
*Mark Sukaiti,Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个Teo型稳态轴对称框架下的精确Kerr-like旋转零红移Morris-Thorne虫洞，通过各向异性源满足爱因斯坦方程，得到具有独立参数(r₀, a, J_ADM)的渐近平直、零ADM质量时空；明确了喉部几何性质（位置、时性、无闭合类时曲线）、坐标适应性，并分析了能层起始与赤道捕获区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造一个既保持Morris-Thorne虫洞基本特性（如零红移、可穿越性），又引入真实旋转效应（类似Kerr黑洞）的精确解，以拓展虫洞物理模型并研究旋转对喉部结构、因果性及能层的影响。

Method: 在Teo型稳态轴对称度规框架下，设定K(r)=r²+a²，利用零角动量观者共动各向异性物质源求解爱因斯坦方程；通过不完全椭圆积分计算参考拖曳；采用拟局域定义（零平均曲率+正面积二阶变分）确定喉面；引入符号适配坐标分析因果结构与渐近行为；基于哈密顿-雅可比方程分析光学极限下的赤道捕获。

Result: 获得一族参数化解(r₀,a,J_ADM)，其中喉半径r_th=√(r₀²−a²)，要求|a|<r₀；喉为类时非视界，两渐近平直端；无闭合类时曲线；能层存在且其起始可确定；赤道捕获区间可在光学近似下给出；J_ADM=ar₀仅为数值演示选取，非场方程约束。

Conclusion: 该Kerr-like旋转虫洞解在几何与物理上自洽：满足零红移、可穿越、渐近平直等关键要求；喉部具有清晰的微分几何定义和良好因果结构；虽依赖有效应力张量而非具体物态方程，但仍为研究旋转虫洞时空结构与可观测效应提供了严格基准模型。

Abstract: We construct an exact Kerr-like rotating extension of the zero-redshift Morris--Thorne wormhole in a Teo-type stationary, axisymmetric ansatz. The Einstein equations for an anisotropic source comoving with zero angular momentum observers fix $\mathcal{K}(r)=r^2+a^2$ and reduce frame dragging to a radial quadrature. For $b(r)=r_0^2/r$, this is evaluated using incomplete elliptic integrals and normalised by the ADM angular momentum. The spacetime is asymptotically flat with zero ADM mass, so $a=J_{\rm ADM}/M_{\rm ADM}$ does not apply. The oblateness length $a$ and angular momentum $J_{\rm ADM}$ remain independent, yielding a family $(r_0,a,J_{\rm ADM})$. The choice $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$ selects only a one-parameter slice for numerical ergoregion and equatorial-capture illustrations; it is not a field-equation constraint. We find $r_{\rm th}=\sqrt{r_0^2-a^2}$, with reality bound $|a|\leq r_0$ and regular range $0\leq |a|<r_0$. In the canonical stationary foliation, the throat is defined quasi-locally as the unique closed surface $S_0$ in the family $S_\ell$ with zero mean curvature and positive area second variation. Moreover, $r=r_{\rm th}$ and $\ell=0$ are coordinate representations. A signed throat-adapted coordinate reveals two asymptotically flat ends, excludes closed timelike curves, and establishes that the throat is timelike, not a horizon. These conclusions hold independently of $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$. The supporting stress tensor, reconstructed from the Einstein tensor, is an effective phenomenological source. We do not claim any microscopic matter Lagrangian or realistic equation of state. We also determine the ergoregion onset. Since the full Hamilton--Jacobi equation is nonseparable, we limit ourselves to derive the optical result for a one-dimensional equatorial capture interval.

</details>


### [155] [Covariant kinetic theory of photons coupled to massive particles in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22837)
*Long Cui,Yi-fan Cai,Xin Hao,Bin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了弯曲时空中光子与有质量粒子耦合演化的协变玻尔兹曼方程，构建了包括辐射吸收/发射、康普顿散射及有质量粒子间散射的协变碰撞积分，验证守恒律，并由H定理导出广义爱因斯坦关系和协变热力学关系；应用于克尔时空吸积盘热辐射，涵盖全局与局域热平衡情形，并推广至多组分任意粒子过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强引力场中，传统平直时空的辐射输运理论不再适用，亟需从第一性原理出发建立适用于一般弯曲时空的协变玻尔兹曼方程，以描述非平衡辐射过程并统一热力学与动力学框架。

Method: 基于广义相对论与动理学理论，构造相空间中协变的粒子分布函数及其演化方程；系统推导光子与有质量粒子各类相互作用（吸收/发射、康普顿散射、粒子间散射）的协变碰撞积分；利用H定理约束微观跃迁率，导出协变细致平衡条件与热力学关系；将理论应用于克尔度规下的吸积盘辐射问题，并推广至多物种过程。

Result: 得到了完整的协变玻尔兹曼方程及相应碰撞积分；确立了弯曲时空中的广义爱因斯坦关系与协变热力学关系（如光子气体状态方程）；成功计算了克尔时空吸积盘在全局平衡与局域热平衡下的热辐射谱；将框架拓展至含任意零质量与有质量粒子的多组分过程。

Conclusion: 该工作为强引力场中非平衡辐射输运提供了严格协变的第一性原理框架，统一了动力学演化与热力学平衡，对高精度天体物理辐射建模（如黑洞吸积、早期宇宙辐射传输）具有基础性意义。

Abstract: This paper develops the covariant Boltzmann equation governing the coupled evolution of photons and massive particles in general curved spacetimes. We construct the collision integrals for photon emission-absorption, Compton scattering, and scattering between massive particles, and show the conservation laws. Requiring the H-theorem to hold determines the relation between the absorption and emission transition rates, which reduces to the Einstein relation in the non-relativistic limit. At equilibrium the entropy production vanishes, yielding the detailed balance condition, from which we obtain the covariant thermodynamic relations of the photon gas. We illustrate these results with the thermal radiation from an accretion disk in Kerr spacetime, first in global equilibrium and then in a Keplerian disk in local thermodynamic equilibrium. We further extend the collision integrals to arbitrary multi-species processes involving both null and massive particles. The formulation treats non-equilibrium radiative processes in strong gravitational fields from first principles.

</details>


### [156] [Charged Klein-Gordon modes on an Ellis wormhole: magnetic confinement and conditional Heun solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22860)
*Abdullah Guvendi,Omar Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外部均匀磁场中，(2+1)维Ellis可穿越虫洞上带电Klein-Gordon场的模式，发现磁场导致径向束缚，并将径向方程归约为合流Heun方程；多项式解仅在特定参数子流形上存在，不具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外部磁场对低维虫洞时空上标量场量子行为的影响，特别是束缚态与能谱结构。

Method: 基于嵌入欧氏空间构造规则方位角规范场，分离Klein-Gordon方程，将径向问题映射为一维Schrodinger型算子，并分析其有效势与可积性（合流Heun类）及多项式截断条件。

Result: 发现非零磁耦合导致全虫洞上的径向束缚；径向方程属合流Heun类；多项式解仅存在于受多重终止条件约束的参数子流形上；推导出相对论能量表达式，并区分了形式连续角动量ℓ解与物理要求的整数方位量子数。

Conclusion: 磁场通过内禀几何与规范结构显著改变标量场谱性质，束缚效应普适，但精确多项式可解性罕见；该模型为研究弯曲时空量子场与拓扑、磁场耦合提供了严格可处理范例。

Abstract: We study charged Klein-Gordon modes on a $(2+1)$-dimensional Ellis traversable wormhole in an external magnetic field defined as uniform in an auxiliary Euclidean embedding space. The spatial geometry is intrinsically catenoidal, while the embedding is used only to construct the external gauge connection. Pullback of the ambient potential yields a regular azimuthal gauge field whose associated intrinsic magnetic scalar reverses sign across the throat. Separation of the Klein-Gordon equation leads to a radial problem that is unitarily equivalent to a one-dimensional Schrödinger operator with a regular effective potential. For nonzero magnetic coupling, the quadratic asymptotic term produces radial confinement on the complete wormhole. The radial equation can be reduced to the confluent-Heun class. Polynomial truncation is possible only on constrained parameter submanifolds determined by simultaneous termination conditions and therefore does not represent the generic confined spectrum. We derive the corresponding relativistic energies and analyze the lowest polynomial sector, while distinguishing the formal continuous-$\ell$ solvability correlations from the integer values of the azimuthal separation constant required by the standard angular periodicity condition.

</details>


### [157] [Probing black holes surrounded by Dust field in Quantum Fluctuation Modified Gravity with twin-peak QPOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22874)
*Kamola Shermatova,Qodir Badalov,Abubakir Shermatov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Bekzod Rahmatov,Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Dilafruz Xujanova,Farhod Nuraliev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子涨落修正引力理论中，被尘埃场包围的静态球对称黑洞解，分析了尘埃场强度参数K与量子涨落参数α对黑洞结构和轨道动力学的影响，并利用双峰准周期振荡（QPO）观测数据对参数进行了限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力效应与普通物质场（如尘埃）共同作用下的黑洞时空结构及其可观测效应，特别是通过X射线双峰QPO约束相关理论参数。

Method: 构建含尘埃场和量子涨落修正项的静态球对称黑洞度规；分析视界结构、有效势、稳定圆轨道（ISCO等）及测试粒子的开普勒与径向振荡频率；建立三种QPO物理模型（相对论进动、扭曲盘、椭圆共振），并结合MCMC方法拟合四颗源的观测数据。

Result: 发现参数K和α对黑洞结构与轨道性质起相反作用：K增大使视界靠近、轨道半径内移、振荡频率升高；α增大则趋于Schwarzschild极限；MCMC拟合给出2σ置信区间：K∈(0,0.5)，α∈(0.3,0.6)。

Conclusion: 该量子涨落修正引力模型能较好解释多源QPO观测，所获参数范围为理论提供了可观测约束，支持该框架作为量子引力低能有效描述的可行性。

Abstract: We study a static, spherically symmetric black hole solution surrounded by a dust field within quantum fluctuation modified gravity, in which the quantum-fluctuation parameter $α$ and the dust-field strength parameter $K$. The horizon structure reveals the two parameters acting in opposite directions: strengthening $K$ draws the inner and outer horizons together until they merge into a single extremal horizon, while a larger $α$ restores the Schwarzschild limit; the same opposing trend extends to the effective potential and the characteristic circular-orbit radii -- the innermost stable circular orbit, the marginally bound orbit, and the marginal circular orbit -- which shift inward with $K$ and outward with $α$, and to the Keplerian and radial epicyclic frequencies of massive test particles. From these frequencies we build three twin-peak quasi-periodic oscillation models -- relativistic precession, warped-disk, and epicyclic-resonance -- along with their $3{:}2$, $4{:}3$, and $5{:}4$ resonance radii, all following the same $K$-$α$ trend. Testing all three models against twin-peak QPO data from two stellar-mass (GRO~J1655$-$40, XTE~J1550$-$564) and two intermediate-mass (M82~X$-$1, NGC~1313~X$-$1) sources through a Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis places best-fit constraints, at the $2σ$ level, of $K\in(0,0.5)$ and $α\in(0.3,0.6)$ across all sources and models.

</details>


### [158] [Time and causality in quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22890)
*Charis Anastopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文从更广阔的视角探讨了量子引力中的时间问题，指出时间概念的困难不仅源于广义相对论的量子化，也早已存在于经典力学、相对论、量子理论和量子场论中；文章系统梳理了主流量子引力路径对时间问题的不同处理方式，并强调其与量子基础及相对论量子信息的关联；最后指出，即使在弱引力和线性化引力等低能近似下，时间问题依然存在且具有操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将量子引力中的时间问题归因于广义相对论的量子化，但作者认为该问题根源更早、更普遍，需回溯各基础理论中‘因果序’、‘时间延展’和‘当下’三方面的时间角色。

Method: 采用概念分析法，分层次考察时间在经典力学、相对论、量子理论和量子场论中的三种本质面向；继而横向比较多种量子引力方案（如正则量子化、路径积分、因果集、扭量理论等）对时间的处理策略；最后聚焦线性化引力与弱场量子系统，检验时间问题的尺度普适性。

Result: 揭示时间难题并非量子引力独有，而是横跨经典到量子、平直到弯曲时空的基础性张力；不同量子引力方案按是否保留外在时间、是否因量子化导致时间失序、或是否将因果/时间结构内建于基本假设而可分类；弱引力系统已显现出可观测、可操作的时间问题。

Conclusion: 时间问题本质上是量子理论、相对论与测量理论深层不兼容的表现，必须在量子基础与相对论量子信息框架下重新审视；它不局限于普朗克尺度，而是一个贯穿所有引力量子化尝试的根本性概念挑战。

Abstract: The problem of time in quantum gravity is often presented as a consequence of applying quantum theory to general relativity. These notes adopt a broader perspective. We first examine how time enters classical mechanics, relativity, quantum theory, and quantum field theory, distinguishing three principal aspects: causal order, temporal duration, and the present. This analysis shows that the conceptual difficulties associated with time do not arise only when gravity is quantised. Tensions between causal structure, clocks, observables, and measurement are already present in the theories from which quantum gravity is constructed. We then survey responses to the problem of time across the principal approaches to quantum gravity. These include canonical and Wheeler-DeWitt quantisation, path-integral and histories formulations, perturbative and background-dependent approaches, causal sets, and twistor theory. The programmes are compared according to whether they retain an external time, render time problematic through quantisation, or incorporate causal or temporal structure into their foundations. Particular emphasis is placed on questions arising from quantum foundations and relativistic quantum information. We conclude by examining linearised gravity and weak-gravity quantum systems. This regime shows that the problem of time is not confined to Planck-scale physics, but arises already in attempts to provide a predictive and operationally meaningful quantum description of spacetime near flat geometry.

</details>


### [159] [Modified Gravity Framework for the Woods-Saxon Inflation: Theoretical Foundations and Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22933)
*Feyzollah Younesizadeh,Davoud Kamani,Younes Younesizadeh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于f(φ)T型非最小耦合修正引力理论的暴胀模型，采用Woods-Saxon势，得到与CMB观测高度一致的ns和r值，并指出该耦合对模型观测量子可行性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个能在暴胀后自然回归标准广义相对论、同时满足当前CMB观测约束（特别是ns和r）的修正引力暴胀模型。

Method: 在f(φ)T型非最小耦合修正引力框架下，引入Woods-Saxon势作为暴胀场势能，分析慢滚动力学并计算原初扰动谱（ns、r）及声速cs。

Result: 获得ns≈0.959–0.966、r<0.01，与Planck和BICEP/Keck数据高度一致；特定耦合函数下声速cs²=1；证实f(φ)T耦合对满足CMB两点统计观测约束不可或缺。

Conclusion: 该f(φ)T修正引力下的Woods-Saxon暴胀模型是观测上可行的，且在所考虑的暴胀观测量范围内自洽有效。

Abstract: We investigate an inflationary scenario within a modified gravity theory that extends the Einstein-Hilbert action through a non-minimal coupling between the inflaton $φ$ and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor $T$. This $f(φ)T$ framework is designed to naturally recover the standard general relativity in the post-inflationary universe. Within this theory, we apply the Woods-Saxon potential. The slow-roll dynamics yields a luminal sound speed $c_s^2=1$ for our specific choice of the coupling function. For the model parameters $a \lesssim 1$ and the modest coupling $β=0.1$, we obtain a scalar spectral index $n_s\approx0.959$--$0.966$ and a tensor-to-scalar ratio $r<0.01$, which reveal a good agreement with the latest Planck and BICEP/Keck data on the $(n_s, r)$ constraints. Crucially, we shall demonstrate that the $f(φ)T$ coupling is essential for the observational viability of the model with respect to the CMB two-point statistics. This work establishes a viable inflationary scenario within the scope of the inflationary observables which are considered here.

</details>


### [160] [Quasinormal modes in the field of a dyon-like dilatonic black hole corresponding to $A_1 + A_1$ Toda chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22999)
*U. S. Kayumov,S. V. Bolokhov,V. D. Ivashchuk,A. N. Malybayev,G. S. Nurbakova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极端稀释双荷黑洞背景下无质量标量场的准正规模，通过eikonal极限和高阶WKB数值分析，验证了在特定极限下结果退化为Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞的已知谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解稀释双荷黑洞背景下的标量场动力学行为，特别是其准正规模特性，以拓展对强引力场中波传播规律的认识，并检验理论模型在极限情况下的自洽性。
