Method: 采用eikonal近似解析推导准正规模，并结合高阶WKB方法进行数值计算，分析不同参数（μ, P₁, P₂）下的频率谱。

Result: 得到了该稀释双荷黑洞中无质量标量场的准正规模表达式；在P₁=P₂=0和P₁=P₂=P>0两种极限下，结果分别精确还原为Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞的eikonal准正规模；给出了前两阶模式的数值WKB结果。

Conclusion: 该稀释双荷黑洞的准正规模具有良好的物理极限行为，支持其作为广义相对论与标量-张量理论中重要可解背景的有效性；eikonal方法在此类复杂黑洞中依然有效。

Abstract: We explore the quasinormal modes of a massless test scalar field in the gravitational background of a non-extremal dilatonic dyonic black hole. The dyon-like black hole solution is considered within a four-dimensional gravitational model that includes two scalar fields and two 2-forms. It is governed by two dilatonic coupling vectors $\vecλ_s$ (with $s=1,2$) that satisfy $\vecλ_1 = \vecλ_2 = \vecλ$ and $\vecλ^2 = 1/2$. We derive and examine the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar test field in the eikonal limit. These modes are governed by the solution parameters $μ> 0$, $P_1 > 0$, and $P_2 > 0$. In the two limiting scenarios -- namely $P_1 = P_2 = 0$ and $P_1 = P_2 = P > 0$ -- our results reduce exactly to the known (eikonal) quasinormal spectra for the Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes, respectively. Numerical higher-order WKB analysis of quasinormal frequencies for two lower levels (for various values of parameters) is also presented.

</details>


### [161] [Optimal all-angle reconstruction of the Hellings-Downs curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23400)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合重建Hellings-Downs曲线的新方法，利用完整脉冲星对协方差矩阵，在每个角度区间内自由拟合振幅，从而得到所有角度下的最优线性无偏估计（BLUE），显著降低了各角度区间的标准差，提升了纳赫兹引力波背景测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证引力波信号起源需精确测量Hellings-Downs（HD）角相关曲线，但传统逐bin加权重建方法未充分利用脉冲星对间协方差信息，导致统计误差偏大。

Method: 基于全脉冲星对协方差矩阵，对HD曲线进行联合重建，每个角度区间设自由振幅参数，构造并求解所有角度下的最佳线性无偏估计（BLUE）。

Result: 在NANOGrav 15年公开数据上，各角度区间标准差最大降低12.9%，中位数降低9.5%；对未来SKAO类PTA，预测最大降幅达36.4%，中位数降幅29.6%。

Conclusion: 该联合BLUE方法能更高效利用PTA数据协方差结构，显著提升HD曲线测量精度，为确认纳赫兹引力波背景的引力波本质提供更强统计检验能力。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) detect nanohertz gravitational waves through spatial correlations between the timing residuals of different pulsars. For an isotropic, unpolarized stochastic background in general relativity, the ensemble-mean correlation follows the Hellings--Downs (HD) curve; measuring this angular pattern tests the gravitational-wave origin of the signal. Standard bin-by-bin reconstructions optimize the weights within each angular bin separately. We reconstruct the curve jointly using the full pulsar-pair covariance, retaining a free amplitude in every bin. The resulting all-angle best linear unbiased estimator minimizes the variance of every bin value and every linear combination of bins. Applied to the public NANOGrav 15 yr data products, our method reduces the bin standard deviations by up to 12.9\%, with a median reduction of 9.5\%. For a future Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO)-like PTA, the predicted reduction reaches 36.4\%, with a median of 29.6\%, enabling significantly more precise measurements of the gravitational-wave background.

</details>


### [162] [Energy-momentum-squared gravity: charged quark star solutions with unified interacting matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23014)
*Ayan Banerjee,Bobur Turimov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Faisal Javed,Gulnoza Palvanova,Sulton Usanov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了能量-动量平方引力（EMSG）框架下带电夸克星的平衡结构，发现电荷显著提升其最大质量和半径，部分模型质量接近3倍太阳质量，符合GW190814事件中次级致密天体的质量范围，并与多个脉冲星及NICER观测数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索带电夸克星在修正引力理论中的结构特性，以解释如GW190814中性质未定的致密天体，并检验修正引力与极端物态联合效应的可观测性。

Method: 采用包含微扰QCD、色超导和奇异夸克质量效应的统一夸克物质状态方程，求解EMSG修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，系统扫描不同EMSG耦合强度和相互作用参数下的带电与中性构型。

Result: 带电构型比中性构型具有更高最大质量（可达≈3 M⊙）和更大半径（compactness≈0.34），中心密度降低10–15%，仍满足因果律；结果与PSR J1614−2230等高质量脉冲星及NICER半径测量一致。

Conclusion: 适度电荷结合非线性物质-几何耦合可在EMSG中支持显著更重的夸克星，凸显带电致密天体在修正引力与多信使天体物理中的潜在观测意义。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium structure of electrically charged quark stars within the framework of energy-momentum-squared gravity (EMSG), adopting a unified interacting quark-matter equation of state that incorporates perturbative QCD corrections, color superconductivity, and finite-strange-quark-mass effects. By solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations over a broad range of EMSG coupling strengths and interaction parameters, we show that the inclusion of electric charge substantially increases both the maximum mass and radius compared with neutral configurations. For strong negative couplings and pronounced interaction strengths, the resulting stellar models reach masses close to $3,M_{\odot}$ with compactness ratios of order $0.34$, placing them within the mass range inferred for the secondary component of GW190814, whose nature remains observationally undetermined. The presence of charge reduces the central density at maximum mass by approximately $10$--$15\%$ while maintaining full compliance with causality requirements. Furthermore, comparisons with observational constraints from PSR J1614$-$2230, J0348$+$0432, J0740$+$6620, J0952$-$0607, and recent NICER radius measurements indicate that charged EMSG configurations remain consistent with current astrophysical bounds over a wide parameter space. These results demonstrate that a moderate electric charge, when combined with nonlinear matter-geometry coupling, can support quark stars that are significantly more massive than those predicted in general relativity, underscoring the potential observational relevance of charged compact objects in modified gravity and multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [163] [Null Geodesics in the Reissner-Nordström Black Hole Spacetime Pierced by a Cosmic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23025)
*Tianxu Huo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了被宇宙弦贯穿的Reissner-Nordström黑洞时空中的类光测地线，根据电荷与质量比|Q|/M将类光测地线分为三类，给出了解析解，并数值模拟了光轨迹；发现宇宙弦参数α增强所有三类测地线的绕行行为，而电荷Q减小、α增大赤道面内的光线偏折角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙弦存在下带电黑洞时空中的光线传播特性，特别是类光测地线的分类、行为及偏折效应。

Method: 通过分析类光测地线运动方程进行分类；求得各类型的解析解；开展数值模拟以分析Q和α对光轨迹的影响；推导赤道面内光线偏折角表达式。

Result: 类光测地线可依|Q|/M分为三类；α增强所有类别中的绕行行为；Q使偏折角减小，α使其增大。

Conclusion: 宇宙弦显著改变Reissner-Nordström黑洞的光线传播性质，其影响（如增强绕行、增大偏折）与黑洞电荷的作用相反，凸显拓扑缺陷在强引力透镜等现象中的重要性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate null geodesics in the spacetime of a Reissner-Nordström black hole pierced by a cosmic string. Analysis of the equations of motion reveals that null geodesics in this conical spacetime can be classified into three main categories, with the classification entirely determined by the ratio $|Q|/M$ (where $Q$ and $M$ denote the black hole charge and mass, respectively). Analytical solutions to the null geodesic equations are obtained for each category. Based on these solutions, we numerically simulate light trajectories and analyze in detail the effects of the black hole charge $Q$ and the cosmic string parameter $α$. It is found that the cosmic string parameter $α$ enhances the winding behavior of null geodesics across all three main categories. Finally, we consider light deflection in the equatorial plane and derive an expression for the deflection angle, revealing that the charge $Q$ decreases the deflection angle, whereas the cosmic string parameter $α$ increases it.

</details>


### [164] [LQG-inspired black hole solutions in (2+1)-dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23044)
*Zijian Shi,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了三种受圈量子引力（LQG）启发的(2+1)维黑洞框架，均显示在引入量子修正后可出现平直、反德西特和德西特三种渐近几何的黑洞解，从而消除了经典情况下(2+1)维与(3+1)维黑洞解的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典(2+1)维广义相对论中仅存在反德西特黑洞（BTZ黑洞）的局限性，探索量子引力效应是否能恢复其他渐近几何的黑洞解。

Method: 构建三种LQG启发的框架：μ̄-scheme圈变量修正、量子Oppenheimer-Snyder坍缩模型、协变有效模型。

Result: 三种方法均一致给出三类渐近几何（平直、反德西特、德西特）的(2+1)维黑洞解。

Conclusion: 引入LQG有效量子修正可消除经典(2+1)维与(3+1)维黑洞解之间的不对称性，表明低维黑洞在量子引力下具有更丰富的结构。

Abstract: It is well known that in (2+1)-dimensional general relativity, black hole solutions exist only in the anti-de Sitter case--the so-called Banados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole. In this letter, we construct, for the first time, three independent LQG-inspired frameworks for (2+1)-dimensional black holes: the $\barμ$-scheme holonomy corrections, the quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder collapse, and a covariant effective model. Remarkably, all three approaches yield the same qualitative picture: when LQG-inspired corrections are included, we find three distinct types of solutions corresponding to asymptotically flat, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter geometries. Our results demonstrate that the asymmetry between classical (2+1)-dimensional and (3+1)-dimensional solutions is eliminated upon incorporating effective quantum gravity effects.

</details>


### [165] [Charged particles and QPO constraints in tidal charge black hole magnetosphere sourced by a current loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23187)
*Ozodbek Abdurakhmonov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Inomjon Ibragimov,Sherzod Djumanov,Nuraliev Farhod,lkhomjon Makhmudov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Randall-Sundrum膜世界模型中，静态潮汐荷黑洞周围由位于最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）上的稳态电流环产生的偶极电磁场中带电粒子的动力学行为；通过求解弯曲时空中的麦克斯韦方程得到精确四势，结合哈密顿-雅可比方程推导有效势并分析ISCO等轨道参数对潮汐荷b和磁耦合ω的依赖；发现偶极场可激发节点进动频率，并基于此构建五种双峰准周期振荡（QPO）模型，利用六颗黑洞X射线双星及Sgr A*的观测QPO数据，通过MCMC方法约束参数，得出中等大小潮汐荷（b≈0.3–1.0）而磁耦合接近零（|ω|≲0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Randall-Sundrum膜世界模型中潮汐荷黑洞周围电磁环境对带电粒子轨道动力学的影响，并利用天文观测到的双峰准周期振荡（QPO）现象约束该模型的关键参数（潮汐荷b与磁耦合ω）。

Method: 在潮汐荷黑洞度规下，求解Maxwell方程获得电流环产生的精确偶极型电磁四势；由Hamilton-Jacobi方程导出带电粒子运动的有效势，计算ISCO半径、角动量、能量与轨道频率；推导三种基本频率（径向、垂直、节点进动），构建RP、WD及ER2–ER4共五种双峰QPO模型；采用Markov链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法拟合六颗黑洞源的观测QPO频率以约束参数(b, ω)。

Result: 发现偶极电流环场可在静态非旋转黑洞背景下激发非零节点进动频率；MCMC分析表明，潮汐荷参数b约为0.3–1.0（中等非零值），而磁耦合参数|ω|不超过0.05（与零一致）；由此给出潮汐荷和磁耦合强度的天文观测上限。

Conclusion: 双峰QPO观测数据支持Randall-Sundrum膜世界模型中存在适度潮汐荷，但未发现显著磁耦合效应；该工作首次将电流环偶极场与潮汐荷黑洞结合用于QPO建模，并为膜世界理论提供了可观测的参数约束。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles in the dipolar electromagnetic field generated by a stationary current loop around a static black hole in the Randall--Sundrum braneworld model. The current loop is located at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of the tidal charged black hole, and the azimuthal electromagnetic four-potential is obtained as an exact analytical solution of the Maxwell equations in the curved background. Starting from the Hamilton--Jacobi equation, we derive the effective potential for charged particle motion in both the interior and exterior regions of the current loop, and analyze the dependence of the ISCO radius, angular momentum, energy, and orbital frequency on the tidal charge parameter $b$ and the magnetic coupling parameter $ω$. We further compute three fundamental frequencies and show that the dipolar loop field revives a nonzero nodal precession frequency even though the background is static and non-rotating. Using these frequencies we construct five twin-peak QPO models (RP, WD, and ER2--ER4) and perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis to constrain the braneworld parameters $(b,ω)$ from the observed twin-peak QPO frequencies of six black hole sources spanning three mass regimes: GRO~J1655--40, XTE~J1550--564, GRS~1915$+$105, H~1743$+$322, M82~X-1, and Sgr~A$^{*}$. Adopting the epicyclic-resonance ER4 model as the representative framework, we find moderate, non-negligible tidal charges $b\simeq0.3$--$1.0$, while the magnetic coupling remains consistent with zero, $|ω|\lesssim0.05$. Our results therefore place upper bounds on the tidal charge and on the magnetic coupling strength from astrophysical QPO data.

</details>


### [166] [Renormalized Dynamical Friction in Black-Hole Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23192)
*Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了黑洞时空中致密天体在物质中运动时的引力自作用的重正化局域运动方程，通过Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分整合环境与度规扰动，得到以质量比和环境密度为展开参数的有效作用量；主导阶上，环境激发的引力尾迹反作用于粒子世界线，对应费曼图含对数紫外发散，需用局域大动量展开结合Barack-Ori模和方案进行重正化，并为理想流体和无碰撞尘埃环境分别给出解析穿刺函数、正则化参数及有限威尔逊系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确建模致密天体（如恒星级黑洞）在星系中心稠密介质中运动时的引力波辐射与轨道演化，需严格处理其与周围物质环境的相互作用所引起的自作用效应，尤其需解决因环境激发引力尾迹导致的紫外发散问题。

Method: 采用弯曲时空Schwinger-Keldysh闭合时间路径积分方法，对环境自由度与度规扰动做路径积分整合，构造因果有效作用量；利用局域大动量展开将弯曲时空推迟格林函数约化为平直切空间形式以分离紫外发散；将发散结构映射至Barack-Ori模和正则化框架，推导理想流体环境下的解析穿刺函数与正则化参数；对无碰撞尘埃情形，通过匹配近区精确解确定有限威尔逊系数。

Result: 获得引力自作用运动方程的完整重正化方案：包括解析穿刺函数、各阶正则化参数，以及关键的有限威尔逊系数（对尘埃情形已由近区匹配唯一确定）；该方案适用于数值自作用计算，且可推广至更一般流体环境。

Conclusion: 本文建立了黑洞时空中致密天体穿越物质环境时引力自作用的系统性重正化理论框架，解决了长期存在的紫外发散与有限物理系数确定难题，为高精度引力波建模（如EMRI系统）提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We derive the renormalized local equation of motion for the gravitational self-force on a compact object moving through matter in a black-hole spacetime. We integrate out the environment and metric perturbations on a Schwinger--Keldysh contour, obtaining a causal effective action given by a sum of curved-spacetime Feynman diagrams organized by powers of the mass ratio and environmental density. At leading order, an environmental wake gravitationally backreacts on the worldline. The corresponding diagram contains a logarithmic ultraviolet divergence that renormalizes the leading dissipative fluid--particle coupling. We isolate the divergence with a local large-momentum expansion, in which the curved-spacetime retarded Green's function reduces to its flat tangent-space form. We translate the resulting singularity into a Barack--Ori mode-sum scheme, deriving an analytic puncture and regularization parameters for an ideal-fluid environment. Mode subtraction fixes the divergent part but not the finite Wilson coefficient. For collisionless dust, we determine this coefficient by matching to an exact near-zone calculation for a black hole moving through the medium. This gives a complete renormalization prescription suitable for numerical self-force calculations.

</details>


### [167] [Instability of naked singularities of perfect fluid under $C^{1,α}$ gravitational perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23357)
*Junbin Li,Tingting Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-欧拉系统球对称自相似坍缩中裸奇点在引力扰动下的不稳定性，证明了初始共形度规的小C^{1,α}扰动（无需对称性）可导致陷获面形成；关键在于声速严格小于光速，使得引力辐射能在流体出现奇点前充分集中并形成陷获面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究裸奇点在引力扰动下的不稳定性，检验宇宙审查假说在爱因斯坦-欧拉系统中的适用性。

Method: 分析球对称自相似坍缩背景下，初始共形度规的小C^{1,α}非对称扰动对时空演化的影响，并利用声速小于光速的物理约束论证引力辐射聚焦与陷获面形成机制。

Result: 证明了小C^{1,α}扰动足以触发陷获面形成，表明该类裸奇点在引力扰动下是不稳定的。

Conclusion: 该结果支持宇宙审查假说——裸奇点在真实物理演化中不可观，因其在扰动下会迅速被视界包裹。

Abstract: We study the instability of the naked singularities arising in the spherically symmetric self-similar collapsing of the Einstein--Euler system under gravitational perturbations. We show that small $C^{1,α}$ perturbations (without any symmetries) of the initial conformal metric lead to trapped surface formation. One key point is that the speed of sound is strictly slower the the speed of light, so the gravitational radiation can become sufficiently concentrated to form a trapped surface before the fluid develops any singularity.

</details>


### [168] [Grey-body factor from correspondence with quasinormal mode for gravitational perturbation of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23434)
*Hyewon Han,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维Reissner-Nordström黑洞的准正规模（QNMs）与灰体因子（GBFs）之间的对应关系，通过数值方法计算了各类引力扰动下的QNMs和GBFs，并验证了该对应关系在不同维度和电荷参数下的适用性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验准正规模与灰体因子之间对应关系在高维带电黑洞中对不同类型扰动（标量±、矢量±、张量）的普适性和有效性，尤其是其是否超越WKB近似所依赖的前提条件。

Method: 采用连分式法计算准正规模，针对Frobenius级数奇点结构，必要时辅以中点积分法；通过数值积分获得精确灰体因子，并与由对应关系推导出的灰体因子进行对比分析。

Result: 对应关系对标量(+)、矢量(+)、矢量(−)和张量扰动效果良好；但对标量(−)扰动，在D>6且电荷较低时偏差显著，高电荷下仍保持一定精度；有趣的是，标量(−)在D=8比D=7更准确；矢量(−)在近极端黑洞中也表现良好，即使势能出现双峰结构。

Conclusion: 该QNMs-GBFs对应关系的有效范围比WKB推导所假设的更广，表明其物理基础可能比现有理论更为深刻。

Abstract: We investigate the correspondence between quasinormal modes (QNMs) and grey-body factors (GBFs) of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black holes. Considering gravitational perturbations of these black holes, we systematically compute the QNMs and GBFs for scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, including the $(+)$ and $(-)$ types of scalar and vector perturbations, to examine the validity of the correspondence. The QNMs are computed using the continued fraction method, and the integration-through-midpoints method is applied when required by the singularity structure of the Frobenius series for each perturbation type and set of black-hole parameters. We evaluate the accuracy of the correspondence for each spacetime dimension $D$ and black-hole charge by comparing the GBFs obtained from the correspondence with those obtained by numerical integration. The correspondence provides a good approximation to the GBFs for the scalar($+$), vector($+$), vector($-$), and tensor perturbations. However, for the scalar($-$) type, the correspondence exhibits pronounced deviations at low to intermediate charges in $D > 6$. At higher charges, it recovers reasonable accuracy despite the effective potential exhibiting a double-peak structure inconsistent with the assumptions underlying the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB)-based derivation of the correspondence. In contrast to the other types, for which the correspondence deteriorates as $D$ increases, the scalar($-$) type is more accurate in $D=8$ than in $D=7$. Furthermore, the correspondence performs well for the vector($-$) perturbation of near-extreme black holes, even though the potential develops a double-peak structure in $D>6$. These results indicate that the correspondence is applicable over a broader range than expected from the assumptions of the WKB approximation.

</details>


### [169] [ACT-compatible Inflation in Scalar Field Coupled $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23511)
*Shin'ichi Nojiri,S. D. Odintsov,V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(Q, R̃)引力框架下构建了与ACT观测约束相容的暴胀模型，并结合f(R)引力耦合标量场进行对比研究，提出了两种满足约束的哈勃参数行为并构造相应模型，同时讨论了暴胀后的再加热过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足ACT（Atacama Cosmology Telescope）对宇宙微波背景辐射的最新观测约束，探索在f(Q, R̃)引力理论中实现可行暴胀模型的可能性，并厘清其与传统f(R)引力耦合标量场模型的动力学差异。

Method: 采用适配f(Q, R̃)理论的重构技术，提出两类满足ACT约束的哈勃速率H(t)演化形式，并分别在f(Q, R̃)引力和f(R)引力耦合标量场框架下构造对应引力模型；同时分析再加热阶段动力学。

Result: 成功构造出两类符合ACT约束的f(Q, R̃)暴胀模型；指出f(Q, R̃)情形下的动力学自由度数目尚不明确，构成与f(R)耦合标量场情形的关键区别；给出了再加热过程的初步讨论。

Conclusion: f(Q, R̃)引力为构建与前沿CMB观测兼容的暴胀模型提供了新途径，但其理论基础（如自由度计数）仍需深入澄清；相较而言，f(R)耦合标量场虽可等效为双标量场爱因斯坦引力，但物理图像更清晰。

Abstract: In this work, we construct models compatible with ACT constraints on inflation in the framework of $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity, with $f(R)$ gravity coupled to a scalar field. The $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory is equivalent to $f(Q, B)$ gravity models or $f(Q, C)$ gravity where $B$ or $C$ is the difference between $Q$ and the scalar curvature $\tilde R$ in Einstein's gravity, so, $B=Q-\tilde R$ or $C=\tilde R - Q$. Using appropriate reconstruction techniques compatible with $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory, we propose two functional behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ that satisfy the constraints, and we construct models that realize these behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ using $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity and $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field. We also discuss the reheating stage after inflation. Although the scenario of $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field is well-known, since the model can be rewritten in the form of Einstein's gravity coupled with two scalar fields, the non-trivial problem occurs in the $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity case, because the number of dynamical degrees of freedom in $f(Q)$ gravity is not settled.

</details>


### [170] [Bi-scalar-tensor theory: a spatially covariant gravity approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23704)
*Jia-Jun Chen,Xian Gao,Tsutomu Kobayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建无鬼多场高阶导数标量-张量理论的新方法，通过将空间协变引力与额外标量场耦合，并利用哈密顿约束分析消除多余自由度，最终得到传播四个自由度的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多场高阶导数标量-张量理论中鬼模（unwanted mode）问题，构造物理上自洽的理论。

Method: 从空间协变引力出发，耦合一个额外标量场，构建空间协变标量场理论；在特定场变量依赖形式下进行哈密顿约束分析，推导退化条件和一致性条件；研究双场逆变变换及其可逆性。

Result: 一般情况下理论传播5个自由度（2个张量+3个标量），引入退化与一致性条件后可消除1个标量鬼模，稳定传播4个自由度；进一步分为是否生成四阶约束的两类情形；给出了双场逆变变换在幺正规范下的表达式及可逆条件。

Conclusion: 该方法为系统构建具有高阶导数的双/多标量-张量理论提供了新路径，确保理论无鬼且自由度可控。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for constructing ghost-free multi-field higher-derivative scalar-tensor theories. The idea is to start from spatially covariant gravity and couple it to an additional scalar field. The resulting spatially covariant scalar field theory can be understood as a bi-scalar-tensor theory in the unitary gauge of one scalar. Within a broad class in which metric velocities enter through the extrinsic curvature and the additional scalar enters through temporal derivatives up to second order, we perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis. We show that the theory generally propagates five physical degrees of freedom (DOFs), i.e., two tensor modes and three scalar modes. One of the scalar modes is the unwanted mode associated with the additional scalar. We then derive a degeneracy condition and an additional consistency condition. When both conditions hold, the unwanted mode is eliminated and the theory propagates four degrees of freedom. The resulting theories fall into two cases, depending on whether the preservation of the tertiary constraint closes without a new constraint or generates a quaternary constraint. We also study the two-field disformal transformation, derive its invertibility condition, and express it in the unitary gauge. Our construction provides a systematic approach to constructing bi- and multi-scalar-tensor theories with higher derivatives.

</details>


### [171] [Black holes in depleted Dehnen dark matter halos with physical inner edges and strong-field observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23750)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Soroush Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个被耗尽Dehnen暗物质晕环绕的静态球对称黑洞时空，描述了黑洞与暗物质晕达到最终平衡态的构型，而非动力学吸积过程；推导了度规函数和红移相的一般表达式，并针对特定cusp指数给出了显式解；分析了能量条件、光子球、黑洞阴影及参数空间，统一涵盖了若干已有解析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述黑洞与暗物质晕共存的最终平衡态几何结构，而非动态吸积过程，需建立一个能同时体现暗物质分布特性（如Dehnen晕的cusp指数）与黑洞基本性质（如视界半径依赖关系）的静态精确解。

Method: 基于广义相对论，构造满足爱因斯坦场方程的静态球对称度规；假设暗物质晕为Dehnen型且在黑洞附近被耗尽，形成真空间隙；通过匹配边界条件确定视界半径与参数的关系；推导并求解度规函数与红移相；分析能量条件、光子球、阴影等强场可观测量。

Result: 得到了适用于任意Dehnen cusp指数γ的度规一般形式，并给出γ=0,1,2,5/2时的显式解；发现主导能量条件要求局部κ≥5/4，而全局可接受性还依赖于γ与晕致密性；稳定Einstein-簇解释需κ≥3；光子球半径、阴影大小等强场观测量受cusp指数和耗尽尺度显著影响。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个统一框架，涵盖Schwarzschild黑洞、弱晕极限及若干已有BH–halo解析解；揭示了暗物质晕结构（特别是cusp与耗尽）对黑洞强引力场可观测效应的关键调控作用，为未来高精度黑洞观测（如EHT）提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We construct a static and spherically symmetric black hole (BH) spacetime surrounded by a depleted Dehnen dark matter (DM) halo. The model describes a final equilibrium configuration rather than dynamical accretion process: the initial halo mass inside a prescribed inner edge is incorporated into the central BH, leaving a vacuum gap between the dressed horizon and the surviving halo. Consequently, the horizon radius depends on the Dehnen cusp index $γ$ and the absorbed halo mass, while the Schwarzschild result is recovered exactly when the halo is removed. We derive general expressions for the metric functions and redshift phase and obtain explicit solutions for $γ=0,1,2,$ and $5/2$. The dominant energy condition imposes the universal local bound $κ\geq5/4$, whereas global admissibility depends additionally on the cusp index and halo compactness. A literal stable Einstein-cluster interpretation requires the stronger condition $κ\geq3$ when stable circular motion is imposed at the halo edge. We also analyze the photon sphere, shadow, and admissible parameter space, demonstrating how the halo cusp and depletion scale affect strong-field observables. Previously studied analytic BH--halo geometries and the Schwarzschild and weak-halo limits follow as special cases of the general construction.

</details>


### [172] [Equivalence of the wave function of the universe in the Einstein and Jordan frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23785)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Sumanta Chakraborty,Soumitra SenGupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量-张量理论和f(R)引力理论在最小超空间量子宇宙学中爱因斯坦框架与约旦框架的等价性问题，发现该等价性依赖于量子哈密顿量的算符排序方案；当量子理论在最小超空间中对点正则变换协变时，两框架等价；对于包括拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算符在内的多种排序，波函数通过对应经典变换相联系，即量子等价成立；但引入任意状态方程参数w的流体钟会破坏等价性，仅辐射流体（w=1/3）例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标量-张量及f(R)引力理论中爱因斯坦与约旦框架在量子宇宙学层面是否真正等价，尤其在最小超空间模型下这一基础问题尚未有普适结论。

Method: 在FLRW宇宙的最小超空间中，采用正则量子化与路径积分两种形式化方法，分析不同算符排序（特别是拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米型）下两框架波函数的变换关系，并引入具有一般状态方程的流体钟考察其对等价性的影响。

Result: 证明若量子理论在最小超空间中对点正则变换协变，则两框架量子等价；对一大类算符排序（含拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算符），波函数按经典构型变换规则关联；加入一般流体钟会破坏等价性，唯辐射流体（w=1/3）保持等价。

Conclusion: 爱因斯坦与约旦框架的量子等价性并非普适，而取决于量子化方案（尤其是算符排序）以及系统是否包含破坏共形不变性的时钟自由度；结果具有模型无关性，适用于一般标量-张量和f(R)理论。

Abstract: We investigate the question of equivalence between the Einstein and Jordan frames for generic scalar-tensor and $f(R)$ gravity theories in the context of minisuperspace quantum cosmology. We consider, particularly, the minisuperspace of a homogeneous and isotropic universe with the Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker metric. We show that the equivalence between the frames depends on the operator ordering scheme of the quantum Hamiltonian: if the quantum theory is covariant under point canonical transformations in the minisuperspace, then the equivalence between the two frames holds. We find that for a large class of operator orderings, including the Laplace-Beltrami operator in the minisuperspace, the wave functions of the universe obtained in the two frames are related by the same transformations that connect the two frames, \textit{i.e.}, the frames are quantum mechanically equivalent. Further, we show that adding a fluid clock with an arbitrary equation of state parameter $w$ to the system breaks the equivalence, except for the case of a radiation fluid ($w=1/3$). All our arguments are presented at a formal level in both canonical and path integral approaches, and for any scalar-tensor, $f(R)$ theories without assuming any particular model.

</details>


### [173] [Signatures in the local interstellar medium of electromagnetic radiation produced by neutron star gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23865)
*Preston Jones,Pragati Pradhan,Douglas Singleton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨中子星是否可能是旅行者号探测到的几kHz等离子体振荡的天体物理源，提出引力波背景中电磁辐射共产生理论作为新解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行者号已持续40多年探测到几kHz的等离子体振荡，且进入本地星际介质后仍存在，但其天体物理起源尚无公认解释；中子星早被假设为候选源，却缺乏能解释该频率的理论机制。

Method: 基于新发展的引力波背景中电磁辐射共产生理论，分析中子星作为该低频振荡源的可能性。

Result: 初步探索表明该理论可能为解释旅行者号观测到的振荡提供新路径，但尚未给出确定性结论或定量验证。

Conclusion: 引力波背景下电磁辐射共产生机制值得进一步研究，或可为解决长期未解的旅行者号低频振荡起源问题提供关键线索。

Abstract: The Voyager spacecraft have been detecting plasma oscillations of a few kHz for over 40 years. However, there is still no widely accepted hypothesis for the astrophysical source of the oscillations. Having entered the local interstellar medium, these detections have persisted, adding to the challenge of identifying the astrophysical source. Neutron stars were one of the earliest hypothesized candidates of the electromagnetic radiation responsible for the detected plasma oscillations, but no theoretical mechanism could be identified to explain the frequency of the oscillations. We explore the recently developed theory of co-production of electromagnetic radiation in gravitational wave backgrounds as a possible source of these low-frequency oscillations and investigate whether neutron stars could be the astrophysical source.

</details>


### [174] [Exact horizon response and charge ensembles of Majumdar--Papapetrou black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23921)
*Jongheon Baek*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维Majumdar-Papapetrou时空中的静电Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，推导出极值黑洞组态的精确电容矩阵，并推广至高维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多中心极端黑洞系统中静电场与几何之间的精确关系，特别是电荷分布、通量分配及自能/自作用力的有限确定性。

Method: 基于Majumdar-Papapetrou度规构造静电势和Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射；利用Frolov-Zelnikov多中心Green函数及其修正形式，结合图论（完全图Laplacian）分析电容矩阵结构；将结果推广至D=n+3维。

Result: 得到显式电容矩阵C_{AB}，揭示其为正对角项加加权Laplacian的结构；提出保持各 horizon 电荷守恒的对称Green函数（含C^{-1}）；导出二元系统中有限且半负定的静电自能差与精确自力差；将结果普适推广至高维，含Gamma函数因子。

Conclusion: 电容矩阵提供了多黑洞静电响应的完整线性刻画；‘共模-差模’分解和电荷守恒Green函数为处理多体引力-电磁耦合问题提供了新框架；所有关键物理量（如自力差）可精确计算，无需紫外截断。

Abstract: We determine the electrostatic Dirichlet-to-Neumann map of an arbitrary four-dimensional Majumdar--Papapetrou spacetime. For a minimally coupled spectator Maxwell field, the potentials of the disconnected extremal horizons may be prescribed independently, and the resulting flux charges are related by an exact capacitance matrix. If the centers have masses $M_A$ and coordinate separations $R_{AB}$, its entries are $C_{AA}=M_A+\sum_{B\ne A}M_AM_B/R_{AB}$ and $C_{AB}=-M_AM_B/R_{AB}$. The matrix is a positive diagonal term plus the weighted Laplacian of the complete graph of horizons, giving an exact common-mode and differential-mode decomposition of the field energy. The grounded Dirichlet Green function shows that a point charge partitions its flux among infinity and the horizons according to elementary harmonic measures. A direct translation of the multicenter Green function of Frolov and Zelnikov gives the diagonal completion used in their published Eq.~(68), whose component-resolved Gauss flux preserves the total horizon charge but generally transfers charge between horizons. The symmetric Green function that preserves every horizon charge instead contains the inverse of the full capacitance matrix. For a binary, the difference gives a finite, negative-semidefinite shift of the electrostatic self-energy and a corresponding exact self-force difference without an additional ultraviolet subtraction. We finally extend the response matrix, flux partition, and fixed-charge Green function to $D=n+3$ dimensions, where the graph weights acquire the universal factor $π^{n/2}/Γ(n/2)$.

</details>


### [175] [Soliton solution of a gravitating chiral-quark soliton model: dynamical fermion and Dirac-sea in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24159)
*Ryoma Mizutani,Daiki Niiyama,Nobuyuki Sawado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称时空下与非线性手征场耦合的爱因斯坦-狄拉克系统，扩展了手征夸克孤子模型（CQSM）至弯曲时空并耦合广义相对论引力，自洽求解后发现狄拉克海会稀释能量动量张量效应，甚至导致ADM质量变负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将手征夸克孤子模型（CQSM）推广到弯曲时空并耦合爱因斯坦引力，以研究引力对费米子动力学质量的影响，特别是狄拉克海在强引力和大动力学质量下的作用。

Method: 在静态球对称时空中建立爱因斯坦-狄拉克系统，将CQSM（含正则化单费米子圈及完整狄拉克海）耦合引力场方程，自洽数值求解该耦合系统，并分析费米子能谱流与ADM质量行为。

Result: 成功自洽求解扩展的CQSM与爱因斯坦方程；发现狄拉克海稀释能量动量张量，导致引力效应出现‘失活点’；当动力学质量M较大（M ∼ ⟨ψ̄ψ⟩）时，狄拉克海主导并诱导出负ADM质量。

Conclusion: 狄拉克海在弯曲时空中的量子真空效应不可忽略，它不仅能削弱局域物质引起的引力响应，还可在大动力学质量下引发反直觉的负ADM质量，揭示了量子场与引力非平庸耦合的新现象。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Einstein-Dirac system based on a Dirac fermion coupled with a nonlinear chiral field on static spherically symmetric spacetime. Gravitational effects on the dynamical mass of fermions are examined. The chiral-quark soliton model (CQSM) originally was a model of hadron inspired by the low-energy regime of large-$N_\textrm{c}$ QCD, realizing localized fermions with the full inclusion of the Dirac-sea, which is derived from a regularized one-fermion loop. We extend the CQSM in spherically symmetric curved spacetime and coupled with the Einstein gravity. We successfully solve the CQSM and the Einstein equation self-consistently, and obtain the spectral flow of the fermion energies and also of the ADM mass. The Dirac-sea dilutes the effect of the energy-momentum tensor, which induces an inactive point for the impact of gravity even when the localizing matter exists. The Dirac-sea effect becomes dominant for larger dynamical mass $M\sim \langle\barψψ\rangle$, leading to the emergence of the negative ADM mass.

</details>


### [176] [Signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes in semiclassical gravity with trace anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24273)
*Yuxiang Zuo,Shiyang Hu,Dan Li,Chen Deng,Guansheng He,Wenbin Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在半经典引力框架下研究了具有A型迹异常的旋转黑洞周围诺维科夫-索恩吸积盘的热辐射特征，发现耦合参数会抑制吸积盘辐射，且高自旋黑洞中该效应更显著，为区分半经典引力与广义相对论提供了观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力通过量子反作用（由耦合参数表征）有望解决黑洞奇点问题，因此需深入研究此类天体周围的吸积性质。

Method: 在具有A型迹异常的半经典引力框架下，系统分析旋转黑洞周围Novikov-Thorne吸积盘的能量流密度、温度分布和热光度等热辐射特征。

Result: 耦合参数的引入抑制吸积盘辐射：能量流密度和温度峰值降低；热光度的峰值及其对应频率均低于广义相对论中的Kerr黑洞，尤其在高自旋情况下差异显著。

Conclusion: 耦合参数增强有效引力场，使稳定圆轨道外移、轨道物质比能升高，从而降低辐射效率、削弱盘发射；该抑制机制为观测区分半经典引力与广义相对论并约束耦合参数提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity provides a promising framework to address the black hole singularity problem through quantum backreaction characterized by a coupling parameter, making it essential to thoroughly explore the accretion properties around such objects. In this paper, within the framework of semiclassical gravity with type-A trace anomaly, we investigate the thermal radiative signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes across diverse parameter spaces, focusing on the energy flux density, temperature profile, and thermal luminosity. Our findings demonstrate that the introduction of the coupling parameter suppresses disk emission, leading to lower peak values of both energy flux density and temperature, as well as lower peak values and corresponding peak frequencies in thermal luminosity compared to their Kerr counterparts in general relativity. In particular, this discrepancy becomes remarkably pronounced when the black hole possesses a high spin. Mechanistically, increasing the coupling parameter reinforces the effective gravitational field strength of the central compact object, which pushes the equatorial stable circular orbits outward and increases the specific energy of the orbiting matter, thereby reducing the radiative efficiency and weakening the overall disk emission. This suppression mechanism offers a practical astrophysical window to distinguish semiclassical gravity from general relativity and to place observational constraints on the coupling parameter via thermal disk spectra.

</details>


### [177] [A novel phenomenological parametrization of dynamical dark energy via matter-gravity coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24361)
*Hrishikesh Chakrabarty,Daniele Malafarina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Markov-Mukhanov作用量与非最小物质-引力耦合的新参数化方法，使宇宙学常数Λ和引力耦合G动力学化，并构建了一个能自然产生动力学暗能量的唯象模型，用于解释DESI观测到的‘幻影穿越’现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释偏离ΛCDM模型的观测现象（如DESI报告的‘幻影穿越’），并从理论上自然导出动力学暗能量，需发展一种能将Λ和G动力学化的新型参数化框架。

Method: 基于Markov-Mukhanov作用量引入非最小物质-引力耦合，使Λ和G成为动力学变量；在此框架下构建简单唯象模型，并利用观测数据约束其自由参数。

Result: 该模型可自然实现低密度下对广义相对论的偏离，从而产生动力学暗能量；其参数可通过当前宇宙学观测约束，并为DESI的‘幻影穿越’提供理论解释。

Conclusion: 所提参数化方案为探索ΛCDM之外的引力与暗能量理论提供了灵活而物理动机明确的新路径，兼具理论自洽性与可观测检验性。

Abstract: We develop a new parametrization for generic departures from the $Λ$CDM model based on the Markov-Mukhanov action with non-minimal matter-gravity coupling. In this approach the cosmological constant $Λ$ and the gravitational coupling $G$ become dynamical with their values determined by the explicit form of the matter-gravity coupling. We use the framework to build a simple phenomenological model where dynamical dark energy is naturally obtained as a consequence of the departure of the theory from classical General Relativity at low densities. We show how the model's free parameters can be constrained from observational data and interpret the tentative observations of `phantom crossing' by the DESI collaboration in view of the proposed formalism.

</details>


### [178] [Joule Thomson expansion of symmergent black holes in extended phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24400)
*Saheb Soroushfar,Sayyed Mehrab Ramezani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称涌现黑洞在扩展相空间中的焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀，发现反转曲线与参数alpha-hat无关，但等焓曲线仍依赖该参数；结果支持全息解释，并构建了自洽的热力学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立对称涌现黑洞在扩展相空间下的热力学框架，探究其焦耳-汤姆逊效应中对称涌现参数的作用及其全息含义。

Method: 采用扩展相空间热力学形式，将压强视为独立变量，结合含alpha-hat修正的对称涌现熵、焓和热力学体积，推导焦耳-汤姆逊系数、反转曲线及等焓曲线。

Result: 焦耳-汤姆逊系数仅依赖于8πP*r_+²，反转曲线P_i = (π/2) * I_i²与alpha-hat无关；等焓曲线则显式依赖alpha-hat；明确给出反转温压，并区分冷却（P > P_i）与加热（P < P_i）区域；结果可作渐近AdS时空的全息解读。

Conclusion: 对称涌现参数影响热力学轨迹但不改变反转边界，为对称涌现黑洞提供了自洽的热力学描述，并揭示了其在全息对偶中的物理意义。

Abstract: We investigate the Joule Thomson expansion of a symmergent black hole within an extended phase-space formalism in which the thermodynamic pressure is introduced as an independent variable. The symmergent entropy contains the parameter alpha-hat, which modifies the enthalpy and thermodynamic volume relative to their standard forms. We show that the Joule Thomson coefficient depends only on the combination 8piP*r_+^2, leading to an inversion curve P_i = (pi/2) * I_i^2 that is independent of alpha-hat. The isenthalpic curves, however, retain a parametric dependence on alpha-hat, indicating that the symmergent sector affects the thermodynamic trajectories but not the inversion boundary itself. The inversion pressure and temperature are derived explicitly, and the cooling regime P > P_i is distinguished from the heating regime P < P_i. Since the effective geometry is asymptotically AdS-like for positive pressure, the results also admit a holographic reading, in which the Joule Thomson expansion corresponds to an isenthalpic process in the dual field theory. The results provide a consistent thermodynamic framework for symmergent black holes and clarify the role of the symmergent parameters in the Joule Thomson process.

</details>


### [179] [Do gravitational waves assist the genuine tripartite entanglement harvesting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24428)
*Xiang-Yue Yu,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在引力波背景下，三个线性排列的Unruh-DeWitt探测器从无质量标量场中采集真实三体纠缠的行为，发现引力波对纠缠采集具有增强或抑制的双重作用，且三体纠缠对引力波频率和探测器间距均呈现非单调响应，比两体纠缠更敏感，有望作为探测引力波引起时空扰动的量子探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力波背景中量子系统（特别是多体纠缠）对时空扰动的响应，以发展基于量子纠缠的引力波探测新方法。

Method: 理论建模与解析计算：构建三个线性排列的Unruh-DeWitt探测器与处于引力波背景中的无质量标量场的局部耦合模型，分析其采集到的真实三体纠缠（genuine tripartite entanglement）随引力波频率和探测器间距的变化行为，并与两体纠缠对比。

Result: 发现真实三体纠缠对引力波频率呈显著非单调响应（抑制–类共振增强–再抑制），在高频极限下趋近于闵可夫斯基时空值；其对探测器间距的依赖也呈现局域增强峰（两体纠缠中不存在）；整体上比两体纠缠对引力波扰动在频率与空间两个维度都更敏感。

Conclusion: 真实三体纠缠可作为高灵敏度量子探针，用于探测由引力波引起的微弱时空扰动。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of genuine tripartite entanglement by three linearly arranged Unruh-DeWitt detectors locally coupled to a massless scalar field in a gravitational wave background. We show that gravitational waves play a dual role in entanglement harvesting, either enhancing or suppressing the harvested entanglement depending on the system parameters. In particular, genuine tripartite entanglement exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic response to the gravitational wave frequency, characterized by successive suppression, enhancement resembling resonance, and renewed suppression, while approaching its corresponding value in Minkowski spacetime in the regime of high frequencies. Its dependence on the detector separation is also nonmonotonic, displaying local enhancement peaks that are absent in the corresponding bipartite entanglement behavior. Compared with bipartite entanglement, genuine tripartite entanglement therefore exhibits a higher sensitivity to gravitational wave perturbations in both the frequency and spatial domains. These results suggest that genuine tripartite entanglement may provide a sensitive quantum probe of spacetime perturbations induced by gravitational waves.

</details>


### [180] [Stationary Rotating Geometries Associated with Nonsingular Pulsating Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28850)
*A. D. Souza,F. A. Brito,J. Spinelly*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Gao-Lu-Shen-Faraoni静态外部几何，利用Azreg-Aïnou形式的Newman-Janis算法构造了一个避免奇点的旋转黑洞模型，并研究了其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决广义相对论中引力坍缩导致的奇点问题，引入受圈量子宇宙学和膜世界启发的高密度修正。

Method: 采用Azreg-Aïnou形式的Newman-Janis算法，将Gao-Lu-Shen-Faraoni静态外部几何推广为旋转轴对称时空，并分析其视界几何、角速度、表面引力与霍金温度等热力学量。

Result: 得到了一个具有Kerr和Schwarzschild极限的旋转黑洞度规；识别出Davies型奇点，并建立了扩展热力学状态空间关系。

Conclusion: 该模型在保留经典黑洞极限的同时，通过量子/膜世界修正避免奇点，并展现出丰富的热力学结构。

Abstract: Since the first exact solutions of General Relativity (GR) were obtained, it became clear that the theory predicts a new class of compact objects: black holes. The Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems show that, under appropriate assumptions, gravitational collapse in GR leads to singular behavior. Motivated by the expectation that such regimes require physics beyond classical GR, effective approaches inspired by Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) and braneworld scenarios introduce high-density corrections. In this work we consider the static exterior geometry introduced by Gao, Lu, Shen, and Faraoni, associated with a comoving interior model in which the singularity is avoided through a sequence of collapse and bounce phases. Using the Newman--Janis algorithm in the Azreg-Aïnou formulation, we construct a stationary rotating exterior extension of this geometry. The resulting line element describes a stationary axisymmetric rotating spacetime whose horizon geometry, angular velocity, surface gravity, and Hawking temperature recover the Kerr and Schwarzschild limits when the quantum/braneworld correction becomes negligible ($l\to 0$). We further analyze the thermal response at fixed rotational state-space parameter, identify Davies-type singular points, and formulate an extended thermodynamic state-space relation.

</details>


### [181] [How Universal Is the Black Hole Zeroth Law?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24648)
*Rajes Ghosh,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞零定律在广义相对论之外的引力理论中的有效性，发现其成立机制并非普适，而依赖于不同理论中场方程在视界上的投影方式；在非Lovelock理论（如广义二次引力）中，零定律由场方程的零-零分量保证，并由此首次在高曲率引力中给出非微扰的严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞零定律在广义相对论外的适用性尚不明确，亟需厘清其在各类修正引力理论中是否普适成立及其物理机制。

Method: 通过分析不同引力理论（广义相对论、f(R)引力、Lovelock理论、广义二次引力等）中场方程在黑洞视界上的投影（如混合分量、零-零分量），识别保障表面引力恒定的具体方程成分。

Result: 发现非Lovelock高曲率引力中零定律由场方程的null-null分量而非传统mixed分量保证；据此首次在非微扰框架下严格证明广义二次引力中零定律成立。

Conclusion: 黑洞零定律并非由普适机制保障，而是可作为探测引力理论热力学结构的锐利探针，并为按热力学机制对修正引力理论进行分类提供新路径。

Abstract: The black hole zeroth law asserts the thermal equilibrium of a stationary Killing horizon, yet its status beyond general relativity remains limited. We demonstrate that its validity is not governed by a universal mechanism: distinct gravitational theories enforce constancy of the surface gravity through different horizon-projections of their field equations. In general relativity, $f(R)$-gravity, and Lovelock theories, the zeroth law is known to follow from the mixed (null-transverse) horizon equations. By contrast, we show that in non-Lovelock theories the zeroth law is enforced by qualitatively different mechanisms. In generalized quadratic gravity, for instance, the null-null component of the field equations dictates its validity. Exploiting this novel diagnostic, we obtain the first exact proof of the zeroth law in the broader landscape of higher-curvature gravity, without relying on a perturbative expansion around general relativity. Our result establishes the zeroth law as a sharp probe of the underlying thermodynamic structure of gravity, and points toward a classification of modified theories according to the mechanism responsible for its validity.

</details>


### [182] [Parameter Estimation of Ringdown Quasinormal Modes with Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14277)
*Momoka Iida,Hayato Motohashi,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自编码器的多分量准正规模（QNM）分析框架，用于从双黑洞并合后的ringdown引力波中可靠提取多个QNM成分，通过物理参数驱动的隐空间实现波形去噪与参数估计一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠提取多个重叠且受噪声干扰的准正规模成分具有挑战性，而QNM的频率和激发因子蕴含了残余Kerr黑洞的关键物理信息。

Method: 构建了一个物理信息引导的自编码器框架，其隐空间被显式训练为表征各QNM成分的物理参数；使用基于高精度Kerr QNM频率与激发因子（含自旋依赖性）构造的可控模型波形进行训练与验证，并按自旋区间划分评估性能。

Result: 在训练自旋范围内，模型能较好重建波形并准确恢复八成分波形中寿命最长的两个QNM成分参数；在特定自旋区间内，可整体较好地恢复全部32个参数；但当验证自旋超出训练范围时性能下降。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了物理信息驱动的自编码器用于预设多成分ringdown波形族推断的可行性，为后续在更真实信号上的测试奠定基础。

Abstract: Ringdown gravitational waves from binary black hole mergers can be modeled as superpositions of quasinormal modes (QNMs), whose frequencies and excitation factors encode properties of the remnant Kerr black hole. Reliable extraction of multiple QNM components is challenging because of mode overlap and noise. We develop an autoencoder-based framework for multi-component QNM analysis, in which the latent space is trained to represent the physical parameters of individual modes, enabling waveform denoising and parameter estimation within a common framework. Using controlled model waveforms constructed as finite sums of Kerr QNMs with recently established high-precision frequencies and excitation factors, including their nontrivial spin dependence near resonant excitation, we assess the method across partitioned spin intervals. The model achieves good in-domain waveform reconstruction and parameter recovery for the two longest-lived components of eight-component input waveforms, while its performance degrades when the validation spins lie far outside the training range. In a selected spin interval, the framework also recovers the 32 parameters of an eight-component waveform with good overall agreement. These results demonstrate the feasibility of physics-informed autoencoder-based inference for a prescribed multi-component ringdown waveform family and motivate further tests with progressively more realistic signals.

</details>


### [183] [Formalizing a unified dynamical system framework for $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24733)
*Saikat Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的动力学系统框架，用于分析f(R)和f(Q)引力理论，摆脱了传统Hubble归一化方法，并通过三类无量纲变量（运动学宇宙学参数、暗能量状态方程w及其导数与物质密度Ω_m、刻画理论函数形状的理论空间参数{mi}）实现系统封闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hubble归一化框架在处理f(R)和f(Q)引力时存在局限，尤其在含辅助变量或难以闭合系统时；本文旨在构建一个更普适、无辅助变量、灵活封闭的动力学系统形式化方法。

Method: 引入三组独立的无量纲变量构建统一动力学系统：(1) 宇宙学运动学参数（如jerk j），(2) 暗能量状态方程w及其对N=ln a的导数与Ω_m，(3) 理论空间参数{mi}；系统可通过三种等价方式闭合：指定具体理论、指定宇宙演化历史、或指定w(z)演化形式；并以Hu-Sawicki f(R)与特定f(Q)模型等为例进行验证与对比分析。

Result: 发现f(Q)引力中从GR物质主导到晚期de Sitter的异宿轨迹具有一般性，而f(R)中该轨迹对初值敏感；在j(z)=1（运动学等价ΛCDM）和w(z)=−1（动力学等价ΛCDM）两种闭合下，GR均为f(Q)的过去吸引子，但非f(R)的过去吸引子。

Conclusion: 该统一框架揭示了f(R)与f(Q)引力在动力学结构上的本质差异：f(Q)更倾向于‘自然’逼近GR早期行为，而f(R)则表现出更强的初值依赖性和偏离GR的趋势；该形式化方法为高阶引力理论的系统比较与观测甄别提供了新工具。

Abstract: Going beyond the traditional Hubble-normalized framework, we formalize a unified dynamical system formulation for $f(R)$ and coincident gauge $f(Q)$ gravity using three distinct sets of dimensionless variables: (1) kinematical cosmographic parameters, (2) the dark energy equation of state parameter $w$ (with its $N=\ln a$ derivatives) and matter density abundance $Ω_m$, and (3) theory space parameters $\{m_i\}$ characterizing the shape of the theory function. Our formulation is free of auxiliary variables, closing via three equivalent ways: (1) specifying a theory, (2) requiring a specific cosmological evolution, or (3) requiring a specific dark energy equation of state evolution.
  Each closure strategy is illustrated with explicit examples. The theory closure approach studies a case of Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ and $f(Q)=-2Λ+Q+β\sqrt{-Q}$ models, where traditional formalisms fail. A heteroclinic trajectory from a GR-matter-dominated epoch to a late-time de-Sitter epoch is general for $f(Q)$, but sensitive to initial conditions for $f(R)$. The cosmographic and equation-of-state closure approaches compare $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravities kinematically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($j(z)=1$) and dynamically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($w(z)=-1$). In both, GR acts as a cosmological past attractor for $f(Q)$ gravity, but not for $f(R)$.

</details>


### [184] [Constraining the geometry of rotating black holes with eikonal QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24824)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯推断比较了基于全局参数化（Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko）的度规特定模型与基于光子球局部度规量的度规无关模型，分析其在黑洞准正规模分析中的先验影响、参数约束能力及对广义相对论偏离的敏感性，并应用于GW250114环-down数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模观测是检验广义相对论的关键手段，但其物理解释依赖于理论模型；需厘清不同建模方式（全局参数化 vs 局部度规量）对参数推断的影响，尤其是先验选择带来的偏差及对理论偏离的识别能力。

Method: 结合校准的几何光学（eikonal）方法与贝叶斯推断：构建两种模型——基于Kerr度规扩展的全局KRZ参数化模型，以及仅依赖光子球处度规函数及其径向导数的局部度规无关模型；开展单参数与多参数联合分析，并映射不同先验在局部度规量上的隐含分布；最后应用于GW250114环-down近似后验。

Result: 均匀先验在KRZ系数上会导致局部度规量先验严重偏斜；对GR相容信号，两模型结果一致；对非GR注入，单参数分析易失效或引入偏差，而多参数分析虽稳健但带来强简并和边缘参数差异；应用于GW250114，两模型均支持Kerr度规。

Conclusion: 局部度规无关建模在物理诠释上更具鲁棒性，但多参数灵活模型面临实用局限；应谨慎选择先验，优先采用能直接反映可观测量（如光子球频率、Lyapunov指数）的建模策略以提升理论检验效力。

Abstract: Quasinormal modes of black holes provide a central avenue for confronting general relativity with gravitational-wave observations from the ringdown stage of compact binary coalescence. However, inferring black-hole properties or the background spacetime requires theory-dependent input. In this work, we connect a calibrated eikonal method, which relates quasinormal mode shifts to local metric properties near the light ring, with the underlying rotating black hole metric. Using Bayesian inference, we compare a metric-specific model based on the global Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko parametrization with a metric-agnostic approach based on local values and radial derivatives of the metric functions at the light ring. For each model, we perform both one-parameter and simultaneous multi-parameter analyses and quantify how their priors map onto the same local quantities. We find that uniform priors on the metric-specific coefficients can induce strongly nonuniform and skewed priors on these local metric quantities. For an injection compatible with general relativity, both models remain consistent with Kerr and yield similar posteriors for the orbital frequency and Lyapunov exponent despite their different induced priors. For injections representing deviations from general relativity, one-parameter analyses can fail to recover the injected local deviations and, in the metric-specific case, can bias the global metric reconstruction. The multi-parameter analyses of both models yield consistent constraints, suggesting sufficient robustness. However, they introduce substantial degeneracies and differences in marginalized parameters, demonstrating practical limitations when considering more flexible models. Finally, we apply the framework to approximate posterior information from the ringdown analysis of GW250114 and find that both models are consistent with the Kerr metric in general relativity.

</details>


### [185] [Boson Star Factory: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24913)
*Romain Gervalle,Jorge Filipe Mónico Delgado,Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho,Carlos Herdeiro,Víctor Jaramillo,Eugen Radu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对非球对称玻色子星（旋转与静态偶极）的数值求解方法进行了系统性比较，使用三种独立代码（有限差分、有限元、谱方法），验证了高精度一致性，并提供了基准数据；还初步探索了物理信息神经网络在Q球求解中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏针对同一物理模型的不同数值方法的系统性比较，尤其是非球对称玻色子星的构造仍具挑战性且各研究组采用自研代码。

Method: 采用三种独立数值代码（FIDISOL/CADSOL-有限差分、FreeFem-有限元、Kadath-谱方法）求解爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登方程，并用多种诊断量评估精度；同时尝试用物理信息神经网络（PINN）构造旋转Q球。

Result: 三种方法对全局观测量（如质量、角动量等）结果一致至约8位有效数字；给出了统一硬件下的运行时间对比和精确的基准物理量；PINN成功实现了旋转Q球的初步构造。

Conclusion: 不同数值方法在非球对称玻色子星建模中高度可靠且可互验，所获基准数据可作为未来研究的标准参考；PINN等新兴方法展现出潜力，但尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Boson stars are self-gravitating solitons of the Einstein-Klein-Gordon equations for a massive complex scalar field, and arguably the simplest horizonless compact objects arising in General Relativity. Beyond spherical symmetry, their construction requires solving a system of nonlinear elliptic partial differential equations, a difficult task per se and that each group has traditionally addressed with its own numerical implementation. A systematic comparison of different methods applied to the same physical model has so far been lacking. In this work, we carry out such a comparison for the two simplest non-spherical configurations: rotating boson stars and static dipolar boson stars. We employ three independent codes, based respectively on finite differences (FIDISOL/CADSOL), finite elements (FreeFem), and spectral methods (Kadath), and assess their accuracy through different diagnostics. The three solvers agree on the global observables to typically eight significant digits. We further compare the runtime of each code on identical hardware and provide precise benchmark values of the relevant physical quantities, intended to serve as reference data for future numerical studies of boson stars. As a further proof of concept, we also present the use of physics-informed neural networks to construct a rotating Q-ball solution.

</details>


### [186] [Observational Features of Nonsingular Rotating Black Holes in the Dark-Energy Dominated Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24926)
*Ramon Torres*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在暗能量主导宇宙中嵌入的非奇异旋转黑洞的阴影特性，发现其阴影面积和垂直直径能清晰反映宇宙学常数的演化效应，从而为区分非奇异黑洞与Kerr/Kerr-dS解提供观测依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESIBAO、CMB和Ia型超新星数据暗示暗能量可能随时间演化；量子场论在弯曲时空及量子引力中跑动引力耦合也为引入曲率依赖的宇宙学项提供了理论动机。

Method: 在相空间中用类光测地线超哈密顿量和Mino型参数直接建模；通过从零角动量观测者反向光线追踪计算黑洞阴影；定义并分析一系列标准阴影观测量（尺寸、形状、位移）。

Result: 阴影面积和垂直直径对跑动宇宙学项极为敏感，可作为清晰观测标识；联合多个强场观测量及不同质量/自旋黑洞的数据，有望严格约束或测量该宇宙学项背后的正则化尺度。

Conclusion: 非奇异旋转黑洞在暗能量宇宙中的阴影特征显著区别于Kerr/Kerr-dS解，为利用下一代EHT等观测手段检验暗能量演化与量子引力效应提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, combined with cosmic microwave background and type-Ia supernova data, suggest that dark energy may evolve with cosmic time. Along a cosmological background, an evolving dark-energy density can be expressed locally as a function of a monotonic curvature invariant, motivating an effective curvature-dependent cosmological term. Moreover, quantum field theory in curved spacetime and approaches to quantum gravity with running gravitational couplings provide complementary motivations for exploring such a term.
  Here we study how the phenomenology of nonsingular rotating black holes embedded in a Universe dominated by dark energy can differ substantially from that of the Kerr and Kerr--de Sitter geometries. The problem is directly formulated in phase space using a null geodesic super-Hamiltonian and a Mino-type parameter. The shadows of the black holes are calculated by backward ray tracing from a zero-angular-momentum observer. We define and analyze a comprehensive set of standard shadow observables, characterizing the size, shape, and displacement of the shadow. We show that the area of the shadow and its vertical diameter provide particularly clear signatures of a running cosmological term. In this way, joint analyses of multiple strong-field observables and of several black holes with different masses and spins could provide a realistic route to tightly constraining, or potentially measuring, the effective regularization scale behind the running of the cosmological term and statistically distinguishing the non-singular black hole geometry from Kerr/Kerr-dS solutions.

</details>


### [187] [Analytical estimates of core optics stray light noise and design requirements for ground based gravitational wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25003)
*M. Andrés-Carcasona,M. Evans*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析框架，用于将探测器灵敏度转化为地面引力波干涉仪核心光学区域的杂散光要求，并通过解析估计多种散射机制、扩展至平面遮光板阵列及确定性鬼束分析，为下一代探测器（如Cosmic Explorer）提供保守的设计依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完整光学布局尚未确定前，需提前给出保守的杂散光设计要求，以保障地面引力波干涉仪的探测灵敏度。

Method: 建立解析框架，推导多种散射机制下的杂散光解析估计式，扩展至平面遮光板阵列，并类比处理被光束截获的确定性鬼束；将其应用于Cosmic Explorer进行验证。

Result: 得到一套紧凑的杂散光预算工具，可用于当前及未来引力波探测器的早期设计研究。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效支持引力波探测器在设计初期进行杂散光控制的定量分析与要求制定，具备工程实用性和前瞻性。

Abstract: We present an analytical framework to translate detector sensitivity into stray light requirements for the core optics region of ground based gravitational wave interferometers. We derive analytical estimates for several relevant scattering regimes, extend the treatment to collections of planar baffles, and obtain an analogous requirement for deterministic ghost beams intercepted by beam dumps. The framework is intended to provide conservative design requirements before a complete optical layout is available. We apply it to Cosmic Explorer to show how these estimates can be used to derive requirements for next generation detectors. The resulting expressions provide a compact set of tools for stray light budgeting and early design studies in current and future gravitational wave detectors.

</details>

### [188] [First-Principles Determination of the QCD Contribution to the Axion-Photon Coupling Using Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22698)
*Tian Lin,Hao-Yang Cheng,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD方法，独立计算了强子对轴子-光子耦合的QCD贡献C_QCD，结果为1.965(35)，并指出与现有NLO手征微扰论结果的表观张力可被调和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不同NLO手征微扰论框架下对轴子-光子耦合QCD贡献C_QCD计算结果存在高达8%差异的问题，提供基于第一性原理的精确基准。

Method: 采用互补于背景场法的格点QCD方法，计算赝标介子到双光子三点函数，结合反常Ward恒等式分离出精确反常项和轻夸克质量压制修正项；使用具有优异手征对称性的域壁费米子，并在两个近物理N_f=2+1组态上进行连续极限外推。

Result: 得到C_QCD^IS = 1.619(30), C_QCD^IB = 0.347(22), 总C_QCD = 1.965(35)；识别出与NLO ChPT结果差异的来源，并证明二者可调和。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了轴子-光子耦合中QCD贡献的首个第一性原理基准，定量检验了不同手征微扰论描述的可靠性。

Abstract: The axion-photon coupling, crucial for experimental axion searches and tests of the strong CP solution, receives a substantial model-independent contribution $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ from QCD dynamics. Next-to-leading-order chiral perturbation theory (NLO ChPT) in different frameworks has yielded puzzling discrepancies of up to $8\%$, motivating precise first-principles calculations. We present an independent lattice QCD determination using a method complementary to the recent background-field calculation. Computing pseudoscalar-to-two-photon three-point functions and exploiting anomalous Ward identities, we separate $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ into an exact anomaly contribution and a light-quark-mass-suppressed correction. The latter is computed using domain-wall fermions, whose excellent chiral symmetry strongly suppresses discretization effects. Working on two near-physical $N_f=2+1$ ensembles with continuum extrapolation, we obtain $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IS}=1.619(30)$ (isospin-symmetric), $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IB}=0.347(22)$ (isospin-breaking), and $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}=1.965(35)$ in total. While direct comparisons with published NLO ChPT predictions reveal apparent tensions, we identify their sources and show that the ChPT results can be reconciled with our lattice determination. Our result provides a first-principles benchmark for the QCD contribution to the axion-photon coupling and a quantitative test of competing ChPT descriptions.

</details>


### [189] [Flowed quark field renormalization in lattice QCD: A Ward-identity approach and its validation using quark bilinears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23379)
*Mei-Qi Gao,Long-cheng Gui,Jun Hua,Jian Liang,Jun Shi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Ward恒等式的非微扰方法来确定流化夸克场的重整化因子Zχ，避免了传统ringed方法的计算困难；该方法利用矢量流归一化和流化/未流化介子两点函数比值，在五组2+1味clover费米子组态上进行了计算，并在多个通道中验证了其有效性；结果与RI/MOM和RI/SMOM等独立方法一致，证明了该方法的可行性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统ringed方法在计算流化夸克场重整化因子Zχ时存在的计算困难，寻求一种更稳健、非微扰的替代方案。

Method: 基于Ward恒等式，结合矢量流归一化及流化与未流化介子两点函数的比值，非微扰地确定流化夸克场重整化因子Zχ；在五组2+1味clover费米子格点组态上实施，并通过多通道（赝标量、标量、轴矢量、张量）进行系统验证。

Result: 成功确定了有限格点间距下的Zχ^V(tf,a)，并在各通道中展现出良好一致性；经连续极限与零流时间外推得到的重整化矩阵元与RI/MOM及RI/SMOM结果一致；不同格距下结果差异随格距变小而减小，反映方法对离散化效应的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提Ward恒等式方法是可行且稳健的，为未来格点QCD中流化费米子算符的非微扰重整化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present a non-perturbative Ward-identity prescription for determining the flowed quark field renormalization factor $Z_χ$, avoiding the computational difficulties of the conventional ringed prescription. The method is based on vector-current normalization and ratios of flowed and unflowed meson two-point functions. We determine the resulting $\mathring{Z}_χ^{V}(t_f,a)$ on five $2+1$-flavor clover ensembles and validate it in the pseudoscalar, scalar, axial-vector, and tensor channels. Renormalized matrix elements obtained through sequential continuum and zero-flow-time extrapolations agree with independent RI/MOM and RI/SMOM determinations. The finite-lattice-spacing bilinear renormalization factors show differences that decrease toward finer lattices, reflecting the different discretization effects of the renormalization methods. The cross-channel agreement demonstrates the viability of the proposed prescription; together, the method and its systematic validation establish a robust foundation for the non-perturbative renormalization of flowed fermionic operators in future lattice calculations.

</details>
